 189: // v4.0c・磯怙莠ｺ蜿檎ｭｾ逧・眠隸ｭ荵会ｼ会ｼ夊ｧ｣譫千悄螳・git 蟄仙多莉､・御ｸ榊★蟄蝉ｸｲ蛹ｹ驟搾ｼ・utover 螳｡譟･ MUST-2・・// PR body / git log --grep / echo 譁・悽蠢・｡ｻ謾ｾ陦鯉ｼ峨ょ翁遖ｻ蠑募捷 + heredoc 蜷取潔 shell 謫堺ｽ懃ｬｦ蛻・ｮｵ縲・function stripQuotedAndHeredoc(cmd) {
 190:   return splitLines(cmd).map((l) => l.replace(/<<-?'?[A-Za-z_]+'?.*$/, '')).join('\n')
 191:     .replace(/'[^']*'/g, ' ')
 192:     .replace(/"(\\.|[^"\\])*"/g, ' ');
 193: }
 194: 
 195: function gitSubcommand(segment) {
 196:   const toks = segment.trim().split(/\s+/);
 197:   if (toks[0] !== 'git') return { sub: '', rest: [] };
 198:   const eatsArg = new Set(['-C', '-c', '--git-dir', '--work-tree', '--namespace', '--exec-path']);
 199:   for (let i = 1; i < toks.length; i++) {
 200:     const t = toks[i];
 201:     if (t.startsWith('-')) { if (eatsArg.has(t)) i++; continue; }
 202:     return { sub: t, rest: toks.slice(i + 1) };
 203:   }
 204:   return { sub: '', rest: [] };
 205: }

 207: // push 蛻､螳夂恚 refspec 閠碁撼 HEAD・・utover 螳｡譟･・唏EAD=main 譌ｶ push feature 蛻・髪蠢・｡ｻ謾ｾ陦鯉ｼ会ｼ・// bare `git push`・域裏 refspec・蛾ｻ倩ｮ､謗ｨ蠖灘燕蛻・髪 竊・蝗樣逵・HEAD縲・function pushTargetsProtected(rest, headProtected) {
 208:   const nonFlags = rest.filter((t) => !t.startsWith('-'));
 209:   const refspecs = nonFlags.slice(1); // 隨ｬ荳荳ｪ髱・flag = remote
 210:   if (refspecs.length === 0) return headProtected;
 211:   return refspecs.some((r) => new RegExp(`(^|:)${PROTECTED_RE}$`).test(r));
 212: }

 214: // v4.7 霍ｨ莉・螟榊粋蜻ｽ莉､諢溽衍・井ｿｮ螳樊ｵ倶ｸ､谺｡ FP・会ｼ壽立螳樒鴫蜿ｪ逕ｨ莨夊ｯ・cwd 蛻､ HEAD・御ｺ取弍
 215: //   竭 `cd /other/repo && git commit`・井ｻ紋ｻ灘ｷｲ蝨ｨ feature 蛻・髪・芽｢ｫ謖画悽莉・HEAD=main 隸ｯ諡ｦ・・//   竭｡ `git checkout -b feat/x && git commit`・亥酔荳ｲ蜈亥・蛻・髪・画潔蛻・困蜑・HEAD 隸ｯ諡ｦ・・OCTOU・峨・// 菫ｮ・壽潔 segment 鬘ｺ蠎剰ｷ溯ｸｪ縲梧怏謨亥ｷ･菴懃岼蠖輔搾ｼ・d / git -C・我ｸ弱悟酔荳ｲ蜀・ｷｲ蛻・芦髱樔ｿ晄侃蛻・髪縲阪・// fail-safe・夂岼蠖戊ｧ｣譫蝉ｸ榊・ / 髱・git 莉・/ 莉ｻ菴募ｼょｸｸ 竊・蝗樣莨夊ｯ・cwd 逧・HEAD・亥叉譌ｧ陦御ｸｺ・悟ｮ∝庄諡ｦ・峨・function resolveDir(base, arg) {
 216:   if (!arg) return base;
 217:   const a = arg.replaceAll('\\', '/').replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '');
 218:   if (/^\/|^[A-Za-z]:\//.test(a)) return a.replace(/\/+$/, '') || a; // 扈晏ｯｹ霍ｯ蠕・  const b = String(base || '.').replaceAll('\\', '/').replace(/\/+$/, '');
 219:   const parts = b.split('/');
 220:   for (const seg of a.split('/')) {
 221:     if (!seg || seg === '.') continue;
 222:     if (seg === '..') { if (parts.length > 1) parts.pop(); continue; }
 223:     parts.push(seg);
 224:   }
 225:   return parts.join('/') || b;
 226: }

 228: function branchGuardBash(ctx) {
 229:   if (!ctx.cmd) process.exit(0);
 230:   const sessionBranch = gitBranch(ctx.cwd);
 231:   const segments = stripQuotedAndHeredoc(ctx.cmd).split(/&&|\|\||;|\||\n/);
 232:   let curDir = ctx.cwd;          // 譛画譜蟾･菴懃岼蠖包ｼ磯囂 cd 謗ｨ霑幢ｼ・  let switchedToSafe = false;    // 蜷御ｸｲ蜀・ｷｲ checkout/switch 蛻ｰ髱樔ｿ晄侃蛻・髪
 233:   const branchCache = new Map();
 234:   const branchOf = (dir) => {
 235:     const k = String(dir);
 236:     if (!branchCache.has(k)) {
 237:       let b = '';
 238:       try {
 239:         // 逶ｮ蠖募ｿ・｡ｻ逵溷ｮ槫ｭ伜惠謇肴潔螳・愛 窶披・蜷ｦ蛻・gitBranch 蜀・Κ莨壼屓騾蛻ｰ縲瑚ｿ帷ｨ・cwd縲搾ｼ・        // 驍｣譏ｯ guard 閾ｪ蟾ｱ逧・ｿ占｡檎岼蠖包ｼ御ｸ手｢ｫ譽蜻ｽ莉､譌蜈ｳ 竊・莨壽ｼ乗協縲Ｇail-safe 逕ｨ莨夊ｯ・HEAD縲・        if (fs.statSync(fsPath(String(dir).replaceAll('\\', '/'))).isDirectory()) b = gitBranch(dir);
 240:       } catch { b = ''; }
 241:       branchCache.set(k, b || sessionBranch);
 242:     }
 243:     return branchCache.get(k);
 244:   };
 245: 
 246:   for (const seg of segments) {
 247:     const toks = seg.trim().split(/\s+/);
 248:     // cd 霍溯ｸｪ・・d - / 譌蜿・竊・荳肴隼・御ｿ晏ｮ茨ｼ・    if (toks[0] === 'cd' && toks[1] && toks[1] !== '-') { curDir = resolveDir(curDir, toks[1]); continue; }
 249:     const { sub, rest } = gitSubcommand(seg);
 250:     // 蜷御ｸｲ蜀・・蛻・髪・・b/-c 譁ｰ蟒ｺ 謌・蛻・芦髱樔ｿ晄侃蛻・髪 竊・蜷守ｻｭ commit 隗・ｸｺ螳牙・・帛・蝗樔ｿ晄侃蛻・髪 竊・螟堺ｽ・    if (sub === 'checkout' || sub === 'switch') {
 251:       const names = rest.filter((t) => !t.startsWith('-'));
 252:       const target = rest.includes('-b') || rest.includes('-c') || rest.includes('-B') ? names[0] : names[0];
 253:       if (target) switchedToSafe = !PROTECTED_BRANCHES.has(target);
 254:       continue;
 255:     }
 256:     // -C <dir> 隕・尠譛ｬ segment 逧・岼蠖・    const ci = toks.indexOf('-C');
 257:     const segDir = ci > 0 && toks[ci + 1] ? resolveDir(curDir, toks[ci + 1]) : curDir;
 258:     const branch = switchedToSafe ? '' : branchOf(segDir);
 259:     const headProtected = PROTECTED_BRANCHES.has(branch);
 260:     let hit = '';
 261:     if ((sub === 'commit' || sub === 'merge') && headProtected) hit = `git ${sub}・・EAD=${branch}・荏;
 262:     if (sub === 'push' && pushTargetsProtected(rest, headProtected)) hit = `git push 竊・菫晄侃蛻・髪 refspec・・EAD=${branch}・荏;
 263:     if (hit) {
 264:       if (env().CTO_MAIN_EDIT_ALLOWED === '1') {
 265:         auditLog(ctx, 'branch-guard', 'main-commit-allowed-emergency', `branch=${branch} cmd=${headBytes(ctx.cmd, 200)}`);
 266:         process.exit(0);
 267:       }
 268:       auditLog(ctx, 'branch-guard', 'main-commit-blocked', `branch=${branch} hit=${hit} cmd=${headBytes(ctx.cmd, 200)}`);
 269:       deny(`尅 體∝ｾ・#8 BLOCKED: ${hit}
 270: 
 271: 蜻ｽ莉､・喀`${headBytes(ctx.cmd, 300)}\`

 281: // v4.0e・井ｿｮ 2026-07-02 隸ｯ諡ｦ + codex ﾂｧ48 蜉蝗ｺﾃ・・会ｼ壼愛螳夂岼譬・枚莉ｶ譏ｯ蜷ｦ關ｽ蝨ｨ蠖灘燕 git 蟾･菴懈大・縲・// 菫晄侃蛻・髪蜿ｪ菫晄侃譛ｬ莉灘ｷ･菴懈・窶・莉灘ｺ灘､匁枚莉ｶ・亥ｦ・~/.claude/.../memory/*.md・我ｸ取悽莉・main 譌蜈ｳ 竊・謾ｾ陦後・// 蟾･菴懈第ｹ = 莉・cwd 謖・`git rev-parse --show-cdup` 逶ｸ蟇ｹ荳顔握・磯撼 --show-toplevel 逧・resolved-real 霍ｯ蠕・ｼ会ｼ・//   - Major-1・・wd 荳ｺ蟄千岼蠖包ｼ会ｼ喞dup 荳顔握蛻ｰ逵滓ｹ 竊・cwd 螟也噪蜷御ｻ捺枚莉ｶ莉肴協・・//   - round-3・・ymlink/junction 蛻ｫ蜷搾ｼ会ｼ壼・遞句●逡吝惠 cwd/file 逧・ｷｯ蠕・ｩｺ髣ｴ・御ｸ榊ｼ募・ real 霍ｯ蠕・竊・蛻ｫ蜷榊燕郛莉榊源驟阪・// canonPath 蠖剃ｸ・哺SYS(/c/)竊貞次逕溽尨隨ｦ + 蜴ｻ蟆ｾ譁懈擒 + Windows 螟ｧ蟆丞・荳肴撫諢滂ｼ・ajor-2・峨・// engine 荳・legacy branch-guard.sh 逧・_canon / cdup 荳顔握蜷梧ｬｾ・御ｿ・parity縲・function canonPath(p) {
 282:   let s = fsPath(String(p).replaceAll('\\', '/')).replace(/\/+$/, '');
 283:   if (process.platform === 'win32') s = s.toLowerCase(); // Windows FS 螟ｧ蟆丞・荳肴撫諢・  return s;
 284: }
 285: function fileInsideWorktree(ctx) {
 286:   const rawFile = String(ctx.filePath).replaceAll('\\', '/');
 287:   if (!/^\/|^[A-Za-z]:\//.test(rawFile)) return true; // 逶ｸ蟇ｹ霍ｯ蠕・竊・逶ｸ蟇ｹ cwd・域￡蝨ｨ蟾･菴懈大・・・  // 莉・cwd 荳顔握 N 螻ょｾ怜ｷ･菴懈第ｹ・・ = cdup 荳ｭ '..' 谿ｵ謨ｰ・会ｼ帛・遞・cwd 遨ｺ髣ｴ・御ｸ・file_path 蜷檎ｩｺ髣ｴ
 288:   let root = String(ctx.cwd || '.').replaceAll('\\', '/').replace(/\/+$/, '');
 289:   const cdup = gitCdup(ctx.cwd);
 290:   if (cdup) {
 291:     const levels = cdup.split('/').filter((s) => s === '..').length;
 292:     for (let i = 0; i < levels; i++) root = root.replace(/\/[^/]+$/, '');
 293:   }
 294:   const base = canonPath(root);
 295:   const f = canonPath(rawFile);
 296:   return f === base || f.startsWith(base + '/');
 297: }

 299: export function branchGuard(ctx) {
 300:   if (ctx.toolName === 'Bash') return branchGuardBash(ctx);
 301:   if (!ctx.filePath) process.exit(0);
 302:   const branch = gitBranch(ctx.cwd);
 303:   if (!branch) process.exit(0); // 髱・git repo / detached 遨ｺ蛟ｼ 竊・謾ｾ陦鯉ｼ井ｸ・bash 荳閾ｴ・・  if (PROTECTED_BRANCHES.has(branch)) {
 304:     // v4.0e・壻ｻ灘ｺ灘､匁枚莉ｶ謾ｾ陦鯉ｼ井ｿ晄侃蛻・髪蜿ｪ諡ｦ譛ｬ莉灘ｷ･菴懈大・逧・峩謗･ Edit/Write・・    if (!fileInsideWorktree(ctx)) {
 305:       auditLog(ctx, 'branch-guard', 'main-edit-outside-repo-allowed', `branch=${branch} file=${ctx.filePath}`);
 306:       process.exit(0);
 307:     }
 308:     if (env().CTO_MAIN_EDIT_ALLOWED === '1') {
 309:       auditLog(ctx, 'branch-guard', 'main-edit-allowed-emergency', `branch=${branch} file=${ctx.filePath}`);
 310:       process.exit(0);

 313:     block(`尅 體∝ｾ・#8 BLOCKED: 蠖灘燕蝨ｨ \`${branch}\` 蛻・髪荳顔峩謗･ Edit
 314:

 320:   }

 371: // 笊絶武笊・trajectory-logger 笊絶武笊撰ｼ・ostToolUse *・夊┳謨・jsonl・孕chema v3.8 蟄苓鰍蜈ｼ螳ｹ・・export function trajectoryLogger(ctx) {
 372:   const cwd = fsPath((ctx.cwd || '.').replaceAll('\\', '/'));

 377:   try { fs.appendFileSync(`${dir}/${localDay()}.jsonl`, line); } catch { /* 髱咎ｻ・*/ }
 378:   process.exit(0);
 379: }

 401: // 笊絶武笊・destructive-action-guard 笊絶武笊撰ｼ・reToolUse Bash・壻ｸ榊庄騾・勘菴・竊・deny JSON・・// SCAN_CMD・壻ｻ・翁遖ｻ heredoc 襍ｷ蟋区・ｮｰ閾ｳ陦悟ｰｾ・・3.11 螳壽｡茨ｼ壼ｼ募捷蜀・ｮｹ菫晉蕗 窶・psql -c "DROP..." 蠢・｡ｻ蜻ｽ荳ｭ・・const FS_PATTERNS = `rm\\s+-rf\\s+["']?/($|\\s|["'])|rm\\s+-rf\\s+["']?~($|\\s|["'])|rm\\s+-rf\\s+["']?\\$HOME|rm\\s+-rf\\s+["']?\\.\\s|rm\\s+-rf\\s+["']?\\*($|\\s)|find\\s+/?\\s.*-delete|>\\s*/dev/sda|mkfs|dd\\s+if=.*of=/dev/`;
 402: const DB_PATTERNS = `${DESTRUCTIVE_SQL_CORE}|psql.*-c.*DROP|mongo.*dropDatabase|redis-cli.*FLUSHALL`;
 403: const CLOUD_PATTERNS = `terraform\\s+destroy|vercel\\s+rm\\s.*--yes|railway\\s+(down|destroy)|supabase\\s+project\\s+delete|aws\\s+s3\\s+rb\\s+["']?s3://.*--force|aws\\s+rds\\s+delete-db-instance|aws\\s+ec2\\s+terminate-instances.*--force|gh\\s+repo\\s+delete|gh\\s+secret\\s+remove|firebase\\s+(use\\s+.*&&.*deploy|projects:delete)|heroku\\s+apps:destroy|fly\\s+apps\\s+destroy|kubectl\\s+delete\\s+(ns|namespace|cluster|all)|docker\\s+system\\s+prune\\s+--all\\s+--volumes`;
 404: const COMBINED_DESTRUCTIVE = new RegExp(`${FS_PATTERNS}|${DB_PATTERNS}|${CLOUD_PATTERNS}`, 'im');
 405: 
 406: export function destructiveActionGuard(ctx) {
 407:   if (ctx.toolName !== 'Bash') process.exit(0);
 408:   if (!ctx.cmd) process.exit(0);
 409:   const scanCmd = splitLines(ctx.cmd).map((l) => l.replace(/<<-?'?[A-Za-z_]+'?.*$/, '')).join('\n');
 410:   // 郤ｯ echo/printf 霎灘・・域裏 shell 謫堺ｽ懃ｬｦ・俄・ 謾ｾ陦鯉ｼ・3.10.2 謨呵ｮｭ・壽枚譛ｬ荳肴弍謇ｧ陦鯉ｼ・  if (/^[ \t]*(echo|printf)[ \t]/m.test(scanCmd) && !/&&|\|\||;|\$\(|\|\s/.test(scanCmd)) {
 411:     process.exit(0);
 412:   }
 413:   if (COMBINED_DESTRUCTIVE.test(scanCmd)) {
 414:     if (env().CTO_DESTRUCTIVE_CONFIRMED === '1') {
 415:       auditLog(ctx, 'destructive-action-guard', 'destructive-action-allowed', `cmd=${headBytes(ctx.cmd, 200)} env=1`);
 416:       process.exit(0);
 417:     }
 418:     auditLog(ctx, 'destructive-action-guard', 'destructive-action-blocked', `cmd=${headBytes(ctx.cmd, 200)}`);
 419:     deny(`尅 v3.10.1 DESTRUCTIVE ACTION BLOCKED
 420: 
 421: 蜻ｽ莉､・喀`${headBytes(ctx.cmd, 300)}\`
 422: 
 423: 蜻ｽ荳ｭ荳榊庄騾・勘菴懈ｨ｡蠑擾ｼ・S 謚ｹ髯､ / DB DROP / 莠題ｵ・ｺ宣楳豈・ｼ峨・蜿り・ｼ唹WASP ASI01 / PocketOS 莠区腐・・ursor+Claude Opus 4.6 蛻髯､逕滉ｺｧ蠎難ｼ荊heregister 2026-01・峨・
 424: 遑ｮ隶､譏ｯ譛画э荳ｺ荵具ｼ啼xport CTO_DESTRUCTIVE_CONFIRMED=1・亥黒谺｡・径udit 豌ｸ荵・ｮｰ蠖包ｼ荏);
 425:   }
 426:   process.exit(0);
 427: }

 429: // 笊絶武笊・mcp-guard 笊絶武笊撰ｼ・reToolUse mcp__.*・壼ｷ･蜈ｷ蜷崎ｯｭ荵・+ SQL 蜀・ｮｹ + filesystem 郤｢郤ｿ驥崎ｷ托ｼ・const DESTRUCTIVE_MCP_TOOL = /_(delete|drop|destroy|purge|wipe)($|_)|_delete_|delete_(branch|project|database|namespace|bucket|file|table|deployment|secret)|apply_migration|reset_branch/i;
 430: const MCP_FS_WRITE = /__(write_file|edit_file|move_file|create_file|create_directory)$/i;
 431: const DESTRUCTIVE_SQL = new RegExp(`${DESTRUCTIVE_SQL_CORE}|\\bUPDATE\\s+[a-z_]+\\s+SET\\b.*(;|$)`, 'im');
 432: const SQL_WHERE_CARVEOUT = /DELETE\s+FROM.*\bWHERE\b|UPDATE\s+.*\bWHERE\b/i;
 433: const SQL_HARD = /\bDROP\b|\bTRUNCATE\b/i;
 434: const MCP_TEST_PATTERN = /\/tests?\/|\/__tests__\/|\.test\.[jt]sx?$|\.spec\.[jt]sx?$|_test\.py$|test_[^/]+\.py$|_test\.go$/;
 435: 
 436: export function mcpGuard(ctx) {
 437:   if (!ctx.toolName.startsWith('mcp__')) process.exit(0);
 438:   let blocked = false;
 439:   let reason = '';
 440: 
 441:   // Check 1・壼ｷ･蜈ｷ蜷・destructive 隸ｭ荵・  if (DESTRUCTIVE_MCP_TOOL.test(ctx.toolName)) {
 442:     blocked = true;
 443:     reason = `MCP 蟾･蜈ｷ蜷榊多荳ｭ destructive 隸ｭ荵・ ${ctx.toolName}`;
 444:   }
 445: 
 446:   // Check 2・啼xecute_sql 邀ｻ query/sql 蜿よ焚蜀・ｮｹ・・4 蜀ｳ隶ｮ・壻ｸ肴沖 description 蜈・焚謐ｮ 窶・  // PreToolUse stdin 荳榊性 MCP 蟾･蜈ｷ description・梧沖謠乗弍 no-op = 陌壼∞螳牙・諢滂ｼ瑚ｧ・learned rule 2026-05-30・・  const sqlText = `${ctx.query}${ctx.query && ctx.sql ? ' ' : ''}${ctx.sql}`;
 447:   if (!blocked && sqlText && DESTRUCTIVE_SQL.test(sqlText)) {
 448:     if (SQL_WHERE_CARVEOUT.test(sqlText) && !SQL_HARD.test(sqlText)) {
 449:       // 蟶ｦ WHERE 逧・DELETE/UPDATE 荳疲裏 DROP/TRUNCATE 竊・carve-out 謾ｾ陦・    } else {
 450:       blocked = true;
 451:       reason = `MCP SQL 蜷ｫ destructive 謫堺ｽ・ ${headBytes(sqlText, 150)}`;
 452:     }
 453:   }
 454: 
 455:   // Check 3・哺CP filesystem 蜀咏ｱｻ蟾･蜈ｷ 竊・驥崎ｷ台ｸ画擅譁・ｻｶ郤｢郤ｿ・・earned rule 2026-05-29・嗔ath 蟄玲ｮｵ・・  if (MCP_FS_WRITE.test(ctx.toolName) && ctx.filePath) {
 456:     const { normFile, normCwd, rel } = normalizePaths(ctx, 'basename');
 457:     if (!blocked && new RegExp('docs/ai-cto/CONSTITUTION\\.md|scripts/forbidden-paths\\.txt').test(`${rel} ${normFile}`)) {
 458:       blocked = true;
 459:       reason = `MCP filesystem 蜀・immutable 譁・ｻｶ: ${rel}・育ｻ戊ｿ・immutable-guard・荏;
 460:     }
 461:     if (!blocked) {
 462:       const { pattern } = forbiddenPattern(normCwd);
 463:       let re = null;
 464:       try { re = pattern ? new RegExp(`(${pattern})`) : null; } catch { re = new RegExp(`(${FORBIDDEN_FALLBACK_PATTERN})`); }
 465:       if (re && re.test(rel) && env().CTO_DOUBLE_SIGNED !== '1') {
 466:         blocked = true;
 467:         reason = `MCP filesystem 蜀・forbidden 霍ｯ蠕・ ${rel}・育ｻ戊ｿ・forbidden-guard・荏;
 468:       }
 469:     }
 470:     if (!blocked && env().CTO_TEST_LOCK_ACK !== '1' && MCP_TEST_PATTERN.test(rel)) {
 471:       blocked = true;
 472:       reason = `MCP filesystem 蜀呎ｵ玖ｯ墓枚莉ｶ: ${rel}・育ｻ戊ｿ・test-lock-guard・個ｧ20.3・荏;
 473:     }
 474:   }
 475: 
 476:   if (blocked) {
 477:     if (env().CTO_MCP_DESTRUCTIVE_CONFIRMED === '1') {
 478:       auditLog(ctx, 'mcp-guard', 'mcp-destructive-allowed', `tool=${ctx.toolName} env=1`);
 479:       process.exit(0);
 480:     }
 481:     auditLog(ctx, 'mcp-guard', 'mcp-destructive-blocked', `tool=${ctx.toolName} reason=${reason}`);
 482:     deny(`尅 v3.11 MCP DESTRUCTIVE BLOCKED
 483: 
 484: ${reason}
 485: 
 486: MCP 蟾･蜈ｷ譚・剞蠕蠕豈・Bash 譖ｴ螟ｧ・育峩霑樒函莠ｧ DB / 莠題ｵ・ｺ撰ｼ会ｼ憩nforcement 蠢・｡ｻ隕・尠・・WASP ASI04・峨・蜿ｪ隸ｻ謫堺ｽ懶ｼ・ist_/get_/search_/SELECT・我ｸ榊女蠖ｱ蜩阪・
 487: 遑ｮ隶､譏ｯ譛画э荳ｺ荵具ｼ啼xport CTO_MCP_DESTRUCTIVE_CONFIRMED=1・亥黒谺｡・径udit 豌ｸ荵・ｮｰ蠖包ｼ荏);
 488:   }
 489:   process.exit(0);
 490: }

  11: export const FORBIDDEN_FALLBACK_PATTERN =
  12:   'auth/|payment/|billing/|secrets/|keys/|migration|crypto/|infra/|terraform/|\\.github/workflows/';
  13: export const DESTRUCTIVE_SQL_CORE =
  14:   '\\bDROP\\s+(TABLE|DATABASE|SCHEMA|INDEX)\\b|\\bTRUNCATE\\b|DELETE\\s+FROM\\s+[a-z_]+\\s*(;|$)';
  15: // hook 扈戊ｿ・ｨ｡蠑擾ｼ壼ｿ・｡ｻ荳・common.sh bypass_patterns() 霎灘・騾仙ｭ苓鰍逶ｸ遲会ｼ・val 073 髞∝ｮ夲ｼ峨・// v4.4b core.hooksPath = 蟷ｿ荵・token・域協荳蛻・署蜿奇ｼ俄披・隸ｻ/蜀・carve-out 扈・3 霓ｮ蟇ｹ謚鈴ｪ瑚ｯ∬ｯ∵・
  16: // static regex 荳榊庄螳牙・蛹ｺ蛻・ｼ悟屓騾蟷ｿ荵・+ 豸郁ｴｹ譁ｹ蜑･蠑募捷蠖剃ｸ蛹厄ｼ郁ｧ・common.sh 豕ｨ驥・/ DECISIONS ADR-010・峨・export const BYPASS_PATTERNS =
  17:   '--no-verify|git\\s+commit\\s+-n($|\\s)|core\\.hooksPath|HUSKY=0|hooks-disable|chmod\\s+-x.*husky|git\\s+stash[^|]*&&[^|]*commit|SKIP=|--allow-empty\\s+--dry-run|git\\s+config.*hooksPath';

  20: // evals / 逕ｨ謌ｷ閼壽悽扈・git-bash 莨蜈･ /c/projects/... 鬟取ｼ cwd・孩in32 Node 逧・fs 蜿ｪ隶､ C:/...縲・// 莉・惠隗ｦ霎ｾ譁・ｻｶ邉ｻ扈・/ 蟄占ｿ帷ｨ区慮鄙ｻ隸托ｼ帛ｭ礼ｬｦ荳ｲ豈泌ｯｹ・亥燕郛蜑･遖ｻ・我ｿ晄戟蜴滓ｷ・井ｸ､萓ｧ蜷碁｣取ｼ・悟翁遖ｻ閾ｪ豢ｽ・峨・export function fsPath(p) {

  28: 
  29: // 笏笏笏 stdin 隗｣譫撰ｼ・ommon.sh read_hook_input 遲我ｻｷ・俄楳笏笏
  30: export function readInput(stdinText) {
  31:   let raw = stdinText;
  32:   if (raw === undefined) {
  33:     try { raw = fs.readFileSync(0, 'utf8'); } catch { raw = ''; }
  34:   }
  35:   let j = {};
  36:   try { j = JSON.parse(raw); } catch { j = {}; } // 隗｣譫仙､ｱ雍･ 竊・蜈ｨ遨ｺ蟄玲ｮｵ 竊・蜷・guard 謾ｾ陦鯉ｼ・ash 蜷瑚ｯｭ荵会ｼ・  if (typeof j !== 'object' || j === null) j = {};
  37:   const ti = (typeof j.tool_input === 'object' && j.tool_input !== null) ? j.tool_input : {};
  38:   const s = (v) => (typeof v === 'string' ? v : '');
  39:   return {
  40:     rawJson: raw,
  41:     toolName: s(j.tool_name),
  42:     // MCP filesystem 逕ｨ tool_input.path 荳肴弍 file_path・・earned rule 2026-05-29・・    filePath: s(ti.file_path) || s(ti.path),
  43:     mcpDest: s(ti.destination),
  44:     cmd: s(ti.command),
  45:     oldString: s(ti.old_string),
  46:     newString: s(ti.new_string),
  47:     content: s(ti.content),
  48:     prompt: s(j.prompt),
  49:     cwd: s(j.cwd),
  50:     sessionId: s(j.session_id),
  51:     event: s(j.hook_event_name),
  52:     query: s(ti.query),
  53:     sql: s(ti.sql),
  54:   };
  55: }

  57: // 笏笏笏 霍ｯ蠕・normalize・・ommon.sh normalize_paths 遲我ｻｷ・俄楳笏笏
  58: // mode 'basename'・壼翁遖ｻ螟ｱ雍･蝗樣 basename・・mmutable / test-lock / eval-gate / mcp red-lines・・// mode 'keep'    ・壼翁遖ｻ螟ｱ雍･菫晉蕗螳梧紛隗・激蛹冶ｷｯ蠕・ｼ・orbidden-guard 窶・'auth/' 遲臥援谿ｵ髴蝨ｨ扈晏ｯｹ霍ｯ蠕・㈹荵溷庄蜻ｽ荳ｭ・・export function normalizePaths(ctx, mode = 'basename') {
  59:   const normFile = ctx.filePath.replaceAll('\\', '/');
  60:   const normCwd = (ctx.cwd || '.').replaceAll('\\', '/');
  61:   let rel = normFile.startsWith(normCwd + '/') ? normFile.slice(normCwd.length + 1) : normFile;
  62:   if (/^\/|^[A-Za-z]:\//.test(rel)) {
  63:     rel = mode === 'keep' ? normFile : path.posix.basename(normFile);
  64:   }
  65:   return { normFile, normCwd, rel, basename: path.posix.basename(normFile) };
  66: }

  68: // 笏笏笏 self vs subproject 譽豬具ｼ・mmutable-guard.sh:21-34 遲我ｻｷ・悟性 env 隕・尠鬘ｺ蠎擾ｼ俄楳笏笏
  69: export function isAiPlaybookSelf(cwd, env = process.env) {
  70:   let self = false;
  71:   const c = fsPath((cwd || '.').replaceAll('\\', '/'));
  72:   try {
  73:     if (fs.statSync(`${c}/playbook/handbook.md`).isFile() && fs.statSync(`${c}/playbook`).isDirectory()) {
  74:       const hb = fs.readFileSync(`${c}/playbook/handbook.md`, 'utf8');
  75:       if (/^## 50\./m.test(hb) || fs.existsSync(`${c}/CTO-PLAYBOOK.md`)) self = true;
  76:     }
  77:   } catch { /* 荳榊ｭ伜惠 竊・髱・self */ }
  78:   // env 隕・尠蝨ｨ閾ｪ蜉ｨ譽豬倶ｹ句錘・婀UBPROJECT 蜈医ヾELF 蜷趣ｼ亥酔譌ｶ隶ｾ 1 譌ｶ SELF 閭・窶・荳・bash 鬘ｺ蠎丈ｸ閾ｴ・・  if (env.CTO_IS_SUBPROJECT === '1') self = false;
  79:   if (env.CTO_IS_AI_PLAYBOOK_SELF === '1') self = true;
  80:   return self;
  81: }

  83: // 笏笏笏 SSOT 隸ｻ蜿厄ｼ・orbidden-guard.sh / mcp-guard.sh 遲我ｻｷ・夂ｼｺ螟ｱ 竊・fallback + 蜻願ｭｦ荳埼撕鮟假ｼ俄楳笏笏
  84: export function forbiddenPattern(normCwd) {
  85:   const ssot = `${fsPath(normCwd)}/scripts/forbidden-paths.txt`;
  86:   try {
  87:     const lines = fs.readFileSync(ssot, 'utf8').split(/\r?\n/).filter((l) => !/^\s*(#|$)/.test(l));
  88:     const p = lines.join('|').replace(/\|+$/, '');
  89:     if (p) return { pattern: p, source: 'ssot' };
  90:   } catch { /* 郛ｺ螟ｱ 竊・fallback */ }
  91:   // v4.0・售SOT 郛ｺ螟ｱ譌ｶ荳榊・譌螢ｰ 窶・stderr 蜻願ｭｦ・井ｸ埼仆譁ｭ・会ｼ御ｿｮ螟・safe-grep 譛ｪ謗･郤ｿ逧・撕鮟伜､ｱ雍･髱｢
  92:   process.stderr.write(`笞・・forbidden-paths SSOT 郛ｺ螟ｱ・・{ssot}・会ｼ御ｽｿ逕ｨ蜀・ｽｮ fallback pattern\n`);
  93:   return { pattern: FORBIDDEN_FALLBACK_PATTERN, source: 'fallback' };
  94: }

  97: // 譁・ｻｶ邀ｻ guard・啼xit 2 + stderr・・lock_with_reason 遲我ｻｷ・・export function block(reason) {
  98:   process.stderr.write(reason + '\n');

 102: // Bash / mcp guard・夂ｴｧ蜃・deny JSON + exit 0・・eny_with_reason 遲我ｻｷ・帛ｯｹ蜀ｲ GitHub #23284・・export function deny(reason) {
 103:   process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({
 104:     hookSpecificOutput: {
 105:       hookEventName: 'PreToolUse',
 106:       permissionDecision: 'deny',
 107:       permissionDecisionReason: reason,
 108:     },
 109:   }) + '\n');
 110:   process.exit(0);
 111: }

 113: // 霓ｯ謠宣・・啾dditionalContext JSON・・ash jq -Rs 霍ｯ蠕・ｭ我ｻｷ 窶・蠑墓梼諱呈怏"jq"・梧裏 stderr 髯咲ｺｧ蛻・髪・・export function remind(text, eventName) {
 114:   process.stdout.write(JSON.stringify({
 115:     hookSpecificOutput: { hookEventName: eventName, additionalContext: text },
 116:   }) + '\n');
 117:   process.exit(0);

 120: // 笏笏笏 audit log・・ommon.sh audit_log 遲我ｻｷ・壼ｭ玲ｮｵ蠎・/ 譛蟆剰ｽｬ荵・/ 逶ｮ蠖慕ｼｺ螟ｱ髱咎ｻ倩ｷｳ霑・ｼ俄楳笏笏
 121: export function isoLocal(d = new Date()) {
 122:   const p = (n) => String(Math.abs(n)).padStart(2, '0');
 123:   const off = -d.getTimezoneOffset();
 124:   const sign = off >= 0 ? '+' : '-';
 125:   return `${d.getFullYear()}-${p(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${p(d.getDate())}` +
 126:     `T${p(d.getHours())}:${p(d.getMinutes())}:${p(d.getSeconds())}` +
 127:     `${sign}${p(Math.trunc(Math.abs(off) / 60))}:${p(Math.abs(off) % 60)}`;
 128: }
 129: 
 130: export function localDay(d = new Date()) {
 131:   const p = (n) => String(n).padStart(2, '0');
 132:   return `${d.getFullYear()}-${p(d.getMonth() + 1)}-${p(d.getDate())}`;
 133: }
 134: 
 135: export function auditLog(ctx, hookName, event, details) {
 136:   const cwd = fsPath((ctx.cwd || '.').replaceAll('\\', '/'));
 137:   const dir = `${cwd}/.claude/agent-logs`;
 138:   try {
 139:     if (!fs.statSync(dir).isDirectory()) return;
 140:   } catch { return; }
 141:   // 荳・bash 逶ｸ蜷檎噪譛蟆剰ｽｬ荵会ｼ井ｻ・" 竊・\"・俄・荳・謾ｹ霑・・御ｿ晄戟蟄苓鰍蜈ｼ螳ｹ
 142:   const safe = String(details).replaceAll('"', '\\"');
 143:   const line = `{"ts":"${isoLocal()}","hook":"${hookName}.sh","event":"${event}","details":"${safe}","session":"${ctx.sessionId}"}\n`;
 144:   try { fs.appendFileSync(`${dir}/${localDay()}.jsonl`, line); } catch { /* 髱咎ｻ假ｼ・ash 2>/dev/null 蜷瑚ｯｭ荵会ｼ・/ }
 145: }
 146: 
 147: // 笏笏笏 hooks-overrides 騾丈ｼ・・ommon.sh maybe_run_override 遲我ｻｷ・啼xec 隸ｭ荵・= 騾丈ｼ stdin + 騾蜃ｺ遐・ｼ俄楳笏笏
 148: export function maybeRunOverride(ctx, hookName) {
 149:   const override = `${fsPath((ctx.cwd || '.').replaceAll('\\', '/'))}/.claude/hooks-overrides/${hookName}.sh`;
 150:   try {
 151:     if (!fs.statSync(override).isFile()) return;
 152:   } catch { return; }
 153:   const r = spawnSync('bash', [override], { input: ctx.rawJson, stdio: ['pipe', 'inherit', 'inherit'] });
 154:   process.exit(r.status ?? 0);
 155: }

 157: // 笏笏笏 git 蛻・髪譟･隸｢・・ranch-guard 逕ｨ・俄楳笏笏
 158: export function gitBranch(cwd) {
 159:   let dir = fsPath((cwd || '.').replaceAll('\\', '/'));
 160:   try { if (!fs.statSync(dir).isDirectory()) dir = '.'; } catch { dir = '.'; } // bash `cd 螟ｱ雍･扈ｧ扈ｭ蜴溷慍` 蜷瑚ｯｭ荵・  const r = spawnSync('git', ['rev-parse', '--abbrev-ref', 'HEAD'], {
 161:     cwd: dir, encoding: 'utf8',
 162:   });
 163:   if (r.status !== 0 || r.error) return '';
 164:   return (r.stdout || '').trim();
 165: }
 166: 
 167: // 笏笏笏 git 蟾･菴懈第ｹ縲檎嶌蟇ｹ荳顔握縲肴衍隸｢・・ranch-guard 霎ｹ逡悟愛譁ｭ逕ｨ・俄楳笏笏
 168: // v4.0e・・odex ﾂｧ48 菫ｮ豁｣ﾃ・・会ｼ夊ｿ泌屓 `git rev-parse --show-cdup`・井ｻ・cwd 蛻ｰ蟾･菴懈第ｹ逧・嶌蟇ｹ `../` 荳ｲ・・// 譬ｹ逶ｮ蠖墓慮荳ｺ遨ｺ・峨ら畑螳・ｻ・cwd 荳顔握蠕怜芦蟾･菴懈第ｹ 窶披・蜈ｨ遞句●逡吝惠 cwd/file_path 逧・ｷｯ蠕・ｩｺ髣ｴ・・// 荳榊ｼ募・ `--show-toplevel` 逧・resolved-real 霍ｯ蠕・ょ凄蛻・symlink/junction 蛻ｫ蜷搾ｼ・wd 逕ｨ蛻ｫ蜷崎ｷｯ蠕・・// toplevel 霑泌屓逵溷ｮ櫁ｷｯ蠕・ｼ我ｼ壼燕郛荳榊源驟・竊・菫晄侃蛻・髪荳頑ｼ乗協・・odex ﾂｧ48 round-3 false-negative・峨・export function gitCdup(cwd) {
 169:   let dir = fsPath((cwd || '.').replaceAll('\\', '/'));
 170:   try { if (!fs.statSync(dir).isDirectory()) dir = '.'; } catch { dir = '.'; }
 171:   const r = spawnSync('git', ['rev-parse', '--show-cdup'], { cwd: dir, encoding: 'utf8' });
 172:   if (r.status !== 0 || r.error) return null; // 髱・git / 螟ｱ雍･ 竊・null・郁ｰ・畑譁ｹ蝗樣 cwd・・  return (r.stdout || '').trim(); // 譬ｹ逶ｮ蠖・= ''・悟凄蛻・'../' 驥榊､・}
 173: 
 174: // 笏笏笏 蟄苓鰍謌ｪ譁ｭ・・ash head -c 遲我ｻｷ・梧潔蟄苓鰍髱槫ｭ礼ｬｦ・俄楳笏笏
 175: export function headBytes(s, n) {
 176:   return Buffer.from(String(s), 'utf8').subarray(0, n).toString('utf8').replace(/・ｽ+$/, '');
 177: }
 178: 
 179: // 笏笏笏 trajectory 閼ｱ謨擾ｼ・rajectory-logger.sh _redact 遲我ｻｷ・・ 譚｡隗・・謖牙ｺ擾ｼ俄楳笏笏
 180: export function redact(s) {
 181:   return String(s)
 182:     .replace(/sk-[A-Za-z0-9_-]{16,}/g, '[REDACTED_SK]')
 183:     .replace(/(ghp|gho|ghs|ghr|github_pat)_[A-Za-z0-9_]{20,}/g, '[REDACTED_GH]')
 184:     .replace(/AKIA[A-Z0-9]{16}/g, '[REDACTED_AWS]')
 185:     .replace(/xox[baprs]-[A-Za-z0-9-]{10,}/g, '[REDACTED_SLACK]')
 186:     .replace(/[Bb]earer[ \t]+[A-Za-z0-9._+/=-]{20,}/g, 'Bearer [REDACTED]')
 187:     .replace(/((api[_-]?key|token|secret|password)["' ]*[:=]["' ]*)[A-Za-z0-9._+/=-]{12,}/gi, '$1[REDACTED]');
 188: }
 189: 
 190: // _escape 遲我ｻｷ・嗷edact 竊・霓ｬ荵・\ 蜈・" 蜷・ 竊・蛻謐｢陦・竊・head -c 500縲る｡ｺ蠎・load-bearing・・val 042・・export function escapeField(s) {
 191:   const redacted = redact(s);
 192:   const escaped = redacted.replaceAll('\\', '\\\\').replaceAll('"', '\\"');
 193:   const noNl = escaped.replace(/\n/g, '');
 194:   return headBytes(noNl, 500);
 195: }

 153: test('branch: symlink 蛻ｫ蜷榊ｷ･菴懈大・譁・ｻｶ莉肴協・・odex ﾂｧ48 round-3 窶・cdup 荳顔握蛛懃蕗 cwd 遨ｺ髣ｴ・御ｸ榊ｼ募・ real 霍ｯ蠕・ｼ・, () => {
 154:   const dir = mainRepo();
 155:   fs.mkdirSync(path.join(dir, 'packages', 'app'), { recursive: true });
 156:   const link = path.join(os.tmpdir(), `guard-link-${path.basename(dir)}`);
 157:   try { fs.symlinkSync(dir, link, 'junction'); } // Windows junction 蜈咲ｮ｡逅・遭・帛､ｱ雍･蛻呵ｷｳ霑・  catch { try { fs.symlinkSync(dir, link, 'dir'); } catch { return; } }
 158:   try {
 159:     // cwd 逕ｨ symlink 霍ｯ蠕・噪蟄千岼蠖包ｼ掲ile 逕ｨ symlink 霍ｯ蠕・竊・show-toplevel 莨夊ｿ泌屓 real 霍ｯ蠕・・貍乗協・嫩dup 荳顔握蛻吩ｻ肴協
 160:     const linkSub = `${link.replaceAll('\\', '/')}/packages/app`;
 161:     const file = `${link.replaceAll('\\', '/')}/README.md`;
 162:     const r = run('branch-guard', { tool_name: 'Write', tool_input: { file_path: file, content: 'x' }, cwd: linkSub });
 163:     assert.equal(r.status, 2);
 164:   } finally { try { fs.rmSync(link, { recursive: true, force: true }); } catch { /* noop */ } }
 165: });

 309: function mainRepo() {

 311:   spawnSync('git', ['init', '-b', 'main'], { cwd: dir });
 312:   spawnSync('git', ['-c', 'user.email=t@t', '-c', 'user.name=t', 'commit', '--allow-empty', '-m', 'init'], { cwd: dir });

 316: test('branch-Bash: main 荳・git commit/merge 竊・deny・嫻eature 蛻・髪 竊・謾ｾ陦・, () => {

 318:   const j = (cmd) => ({ tool_name: 'Bash', tool_input: { command: cmd }, cwd: dir });
 319:   assert.ok(run('branch-guard', j('git commit -m x')).stdout.includes(DENY_MARK));
 320:   assert.ok(run('branch-guard', j('git merge feat/x')).stdout.includes(DENY_MARK));
 321:   spawnSync('git', ['checkout', '-b', 'feat/y'], { cwd: dir });

 323: });

 325: test('branch-Bash: push refspec 蛻､螳夲ｼ・EAD=main 謗ｨ feature 蠢・｡ｻ謾ｾ陦・窶・FP 遏ｩ髦ｵ譬ｸ蠢・ｼ・, () => {
 326:   const dir = mainRepo();
 327:   const j = (cmd) => ({ tool_name: 'Bash', tool_input: { command: cmd }, cwd: dir });
 328:   assert.ok(run('branch-guard', j('git push origin main')).stdout.includes(DENY_MARK));
 329:   assert.ok(run('branch-guard', j('git push -u origin HEAD:master')).stdout.includes(DENY_MARK));
 330:   assert.ok(run('branch-guard', j('git push')).stdout.includes(DENY_MARK)); // bare push・粂EAD=main
 331:   assert.equal(run('branch-guard', j('git push origin feature-x')).stdout, ''); // 蜈ｳ髞ｮ FP 譯井ｾ・  assert.equal(run('branch-guard', j('git push -u origin feat/v4.0c-guard-semantics')).stdout, '');
 332: });

 334: test('branch-Bash: v4.7 霍ｨ莉・cd / git -C 窶披・謖臥岼譬・ｻ・HEAD 蛻､螳夲ｼ井ｿｮ螳樊ｵ・FP・・, () => {
 335:   const mainDir = mainRepo();      // 莨夊ｯ・cwd・亥惠 main・・  const featDir = mainRepo();      // 蜿ｦ荳莉・  spawnSync('git', ['checkout', '-b', 'feat/x'], { cwd: featDir });
 336:   const f = featDir.replaceAll('\\', '/');
 337:   const m = mainDir.replaceAll('\\', '/');
 338:   const j = (cmd) => ({ tool_name: 'Bash', tool_input: { command: cmd }, cwd: mainDir });
 339:   // 莉紋ｻ灘ｷｲ蝨ｨ feature 蛻・髪 竊・謾ｾ陦鯉ｼ域立螳樒鴫謖我ｼ夊ｯ・cwd 逧・main 隸ｯ諡ｦ・・  assert.equal(run('branch-guard', j(`cd ${f} && git commit -m x`)).stdout, '', 'cd 蛻ｰ feature 莉灘ｺ疲叛陦・);
 340:   assert.equal(run('branch-guard', j(`git -C ${f} commit -m x`)).stdout, '', 'git -C feature 莉灘ｺ疲叛陦・);
 341:   // 莉紋ｻ灘惠 main 竊・莉肴協・井ｸ崎・蝗荳ｺ霍ｨ莉灘ｰｱ謾ｾ豌ｴ・・  assert.ok(run('branch-guard', j(`cd ${m} && git commit -m x`)).stdout.includes(DENY_MARK), 'cd 蛻ｰ main 莉謎ｻ肴協');
 342:   assert.ok(run('branch-guard', j(`git -C ${m} commit -m x`)).stdout.includes(DENY_MARK), 'git -C main 莉謎ｻ肴協');
 343:   // cd 蝗・main 莉楢ｦ・尠蜑堺ｸ荳ｪ cd 竊・莉肴協・磯｡ｺ蠎剰ｷ溯ｸｪ・碁亟扈戊ｿ・ｼ・  assert.ok(run('branch-guard', j(`cd ${f} && cd ${m} && git commit -m x`)).stdout.includes(DENY_MARK), 'cd 體ｾ譛ｫ遶ｯ荳ｺ main 莉肴協');
 344:   // 逶ｮ蠖穂ｸ榊ｭ伜惠 竊・fail-safe 蝗樣莨夊ｯ・HEAD・・ain・俄・ 諡ｦ
 345:   assert.ok(run('branch-guard', j('cd /no/such/dir && git commit -m x')).stdout.includes(DENY_MARK), '逶ｮ蠖穂ｸ榊ｭ伜惠蠎・fail-safe 諡ｦ');
 346: });

 348: test('branch-Bash: v4.7 蜷御ｸｲ checkout -b 蜷・commit 竊・謾ｾ陦鯉ｼ帛・蝗樔ｿ晄侃蛻・髪 竊・莉肴協', () => {
 349:   const dir = mainRepo();

 352:   assert.equal(run('branch-guard', j('git switch -c feat/y && git commit -m x')).stdout, '', 'switch -c 蜷檎炊');
 353:   assert.ok(run('branch-guard', j('git checkout -b feat/x && git checkout main && git commit -m x')).stdout.includes(DENY_MARK), '蛻・屓 main 莉肴協');

 355: });

 357: test('branch-Bash: 譁・悽/蠑募捷/heredoc/髱樊鴬陦・git 蟄仙多莉､蜈ｨ驛ｨ謾ｾ陦鯉ｼ・earned rule 2026-05-20・・, () => {
 358:   const dir = mainRepo();

 372: });

 374: test('self-protection: Write 隕・・譌｢譛・guard 竊・exit 2・帛黒 Edit / 譁ｰ譁・ｻｶ / AMEND 竊・謾ｾ陦・, () => {
 375:   const dir = tmpProject();

 378:   const wj = (file) => ({ tool_name: 'Write', tool_input: { file_path: `${dir.replaceAll('\\', '/')}/${file}`, content: 'x' }, cwd: dir });

 381:   assert.equal(run('immutable-guard', wj('.claude/hooks/engine/new-helper.mjs')).status, 0); // 譁ｰ譁・ｻｶ謾ｾ陦・  const ej = { tool_name: 'Edit', tool_input: { file_path: `${dir.replaceAll('\\', '/')}/.claude/hooks/forbidden-guard.sh`, old_string: 'exit 0', new_string: 'exit 0 # fix' }, cwd: dir };
 382:   assert.equal(run('immutable-guard', ej).status, 0); // 蜊・Edit 邊ｾ菫ｮ荳榊女髦ｻ

 393: test('engine: hooks-overrides 騾丈ｼ・・xec 隸ｭ荵会ｼ夐蜃ｺ遐∽ｼ謦ｭ・・, () => {

---.codex/hooks/lib/common.sh---
   1: #!/usr/bin/env bash
   2: # v3.8 hook 蜈ｬ逕ｨ蠎・窶・stdin JSON 隗｣譫舌・剄郤ｧ縲‘nforcement helpers
   3: #
   4: # 蜈ｳ髞ｮ・咾laude Code hook input 譏ｯ stdin JSON・井ｸ肴弍 env var・・# Windows git-bash 鮟倩ｮ､譌 jq 竊・逕ｨ sed/grep fallback 隗｣譫・# 譁・｡｣・喇ttps://code.claude.com/docs/en/hooks
   5: set -uo pipefail
   6: 
   7: # 譽豬・jq・帑ｸ榊ｭ伜惠逕ｨ fallback parser
   8: HAS_JQ=0
   9: command -v jq >/dev/null 2>&1 && HAS_JQ=1
  10: 
  11: # 逕ｨ jq 謌・sed/grep 謠仙叙 JSON 蟄玲ｮｵ・磯｡ｶ螻・OR 蠏悟･暦ｼ・# 逕ｨ豕・ _json_get "$JSON" "tool_name"  (鬘ｶ螻・
  12: #       _json_get "$JSON" "tool_input.file_path"  (蠏悟･・
  13: _json_get() {
  14:   local json="$1"
  15:   local path="$2"
  16:   if [ "$HAS_JQ" = "1" ]; then
  17:     echo "$json" | jq -r ".${path} // empty" 2>/dev/null
  18:   else
  19:     # sed fallback・壼､・炊 "key": "value" 讓｡蠑・    # 謾ｯ謖・1 螻ょｵ悟･暦ｼ嗾ool_input.file_path 竊・謇ｾ "file_path"
  20:     local key="${path##*.}"  # 蜿匁怙蜷惹ｸ谿ｵ
  21:     # v3.11 fix・磯｣櫁ｽｮ隨ｬ 8 霓ｮ architect-critic 體ｾ・会ｼ壼､・炊 JSON 霓ｬ荵牙ｼ募捷 \"
  22:     # 譌ｧ regex [^"]* 驕・芦蜻ｽ莉､蜷ｫ \" (螯・psql -c "DROP DATABASE") 謠仙燕謌ｪ譁ｭ 竊・    # destructive/forbidden guard 蝨ｨ譌 jq(Windows) 邇ｯ蠅・ｼ剰ｿ・ｼ募捷蜀・ｮｹ縲ょｮ牙・ bug縲・    # 譁ｰ regex (\\.|[^"\\])* 豁｣遑ｮ蜷樊脂 \" \\ 遲芽ｽｬ荵牙ｺ丞・・悟・霑伜次縲・    # v3.12 fix・育悄 eval executor 謚灘芦 v3.11 regression・会ｼ・    # 蜿ｪ霑伜次 \" 蜥・\\・・*菫晉蕗蟄鈴擇驥・\n \t**縲ゆｹ句燕 s/\\n/ /g 謚・\n 霓ｬ遨ｺ譬ｼ・檎ｴ蝮丈ｺ・    # immutable-guard 蟇ｹ forbidden-paths.txt 螟夊｡・old/new 逧・ｯ泌ｯｹ・育ｺ｢郤ｿ 3 閾ｪ蟾ｱ printf %b
  23:     # 霑伜次 \n竊呈困陦鯉ｼ幄凶霑咎㈹蜈郁ｽｬ遨ｺ譬ｼ・瑚ｿ伜次螟ｱ謨・竊・蛻譚｡逶ｮ譽豬句､ｱ轣ｵ・悟ｮ牙・ regression・峨・    # 蜻ｽ莉､蝨ｺ譎ｯ荳榊女蠖ｱ蜩搾ｼ單estructive-guard 蜈・tr -d 謐｢陦・+ heredoc 蜑･遖ｻ・悟ｭ鈴擇驥・\n 荳榊ｽｱ蜩・grep縲・    echo "$json" | tr -d '\n' | \
  24:       sed -nE "s/.*\"${key}\"[[:space:]]*:[[:space:]]*\"((\\\\.|[^\"\\\\])*)\".*/\\1/p" | \
  25:       head -1 | \
  26:       sed -E 's/\\"/"/g; s/\\\\/\\/g'
  27:   fi
  28: }
  29: 
  30: # 隸ｻ stdin JSON 謠仙叙蟶ｸ逕ｨ蟄玲ｮｵ
  31: read_hook_input() {
  32:   HOOK_JSON=$(cat 2>/dev/null || echo '{}')
  33:   HOOK_TOOL_NAME=$(_json_get "$HOOK_JSON" "tool_name")
  34:   HOOK_FILE_PATH=$(_json_get "$HOOK_JSON" "tool_input.file_path")
  35:   # v3.11.1・磯｣櫁ｽｮ隨ｬ 8 霓ｮ architect-critic 蜿醍鴫・会ｼ哺CP filesystem 蟾･蜈ｷ逕ｨ tool_input.path
  36:   # 荳肴弍 file_path縲ゆｸ榊叙螳・竊・mcp__filesystem__write_file 謾ｹ CLAUDE.md 扈戊ｿ・園譛臥ｺ｢郤ｿ縲・  # 闍･ file_path 荳ｺ遨ｺ蛻吝屓騾蜿・path・・CP filesystem・・ source・・ove 逧・ｺ撰ｼ峨・  if [ -z "$HOOK_FILE_PATH" ]; then
  37:     HOOK_FILE_PATH=$(_json_get "$HOOK_JSON" "tool_input.path")
  38:   fi
  39:   HOOK_MCP_DEST=$(_json_get "$HOOK_JSON" "tool_input.destination")
  40:   HOOK_BASH_CMD=$(_json_get "$HOOK_JSON" "tool_input.command")
  41:   HOOK_OLD_STRING=$(_json_get "$HOOK_JSON" "tool_input.old_string")
  42:   HOOK_NEW_STRING=$(_json_get "$HOOK_JSON" "tool_input.new_string")
  43:   HOOK_CONTENT=$(_json_get "$HOOK_JSON" "tool_input.content")
  44:   HOOK_PROMPT=$(_json_get "$HOOK_JSON" "prompt")
  45:   HOOK_CWD=$(_json_get "$HOOK_JSON" "cwd")
  46:   HOOK_SESSION_ID=$(_json_get "$HOOK_JSON" "session_id")
  47:   HOOK_EVENT=$(_json_get "$HOOK_JSON" "hook_event_name")
  48:   export HOOK_JSON HOOK_TOOL_NAME HOOK_FILE_PATH HOOK_BASH_CMD \
  49:          HOOK_OLD_STRING HOOK_NEW_STRING HOOK_CONTENT HOOK_PROMPT \
  50:          HOOK_CWD HOOK_SESSION_ID HOOK_EVENT HAS_JQ
  51: }
  52: 
  53: # v3.11・磯｣櫁ｽｮ隨ｬ 7 霓ｮ team 霑ｭ莉｣・会ｼ夂ｻ滉ｸ霍ｯ蠕・normalize helper
  54: # 隗｣蜀ｳ Windows 蜿肴万譚霍ｯ蠕・翁遖ｻ髱咎ｻ伜､ｱ謨茨ｼ・earned rule 2026-05-12 隴ｦ蜻顔噪蜷梧ｺ・bug・・# v3.9.1/.2 菫ｮ莠・forbidden/immutable・御ｽ・test-lock/eval-gate 貍・sweep 窶・譛ｬ helper 扈滉ｸ
  55: #
  56: # 逕ｨ豕包ｼ嗷ead_hook_input 蜷手ｰ・normalize_paths・悟ｾ怜芦・・#   HOOK_NORM_FILE 窶・蜿肴万譚霓ｬ豁｣譁懈擒逧・ｻ晏ｯｹ霍ｯ蠕・#   HOOK_NORM_CWD  窶・蜷御ｸ・cwd
  57: #   HOOK_REL       窶・逶ｸ蟇ｹ霍ｯ蠕・ｼ亥翁遖ｻ cwd 蜑咲ｼ・帛翁遖ｻ螟ｱ雍･逕ｨ basename・・#   HOOK_BASENAME  窶・譁・ｻｶ蜷・normalize_paths() {
  58:   HOOK_NORM_FILE="${HOOK_FILE_PATH//\\//}"
  59:   local cwd="${HOOK_CWD:-.}"
  60:   HOOK_NORM_CWD="${cwd//\\//}"
  61:   HOOK_REL="${HOOK_NORM_FILE#${HOOK_NORM_CWD}/}"
  62:   # 蜑･遖ｻ螟ｱ雍･・井ｸ榊惠 cwd 蜀・/ 扈晏ｯｹ霍ｯ蠕・ｮ狗蕗・俄・ basename 蜈懷ｺ・  case "$HOOK_REL" in
  63:     /*|[A-Za-z]:/*) HOOK_REL=$(basename "$HOOK_NORM_FILE") ;;
  64:   esac
  65:   HOOK_BASENAME=$(basename "$HOOK_NORM_FILE")
  66:   export HOOK_NORM_FILE HOOK_NORM_CWD HOOK_REL HOOK_BASENAME
  67: }
  68: 
  69: # 遑ｬ髦ｻ豁｢・啼xit 2 + stderr・・laude 莨夊ｯｻ stderr 蠖謎ｽ憺漠隸ｯ蜿埼ｦ茨ｼ・# 譁・ｻｶ邀ｻ蟾･蜈ｷ・・dit/Write/MultiEdit・臥噪 PreToolUse 逕ｨ豁､窶披泌ｮ樊ｵ句庄髱諡ｦ謌ｪ縲・block_with_reason() {
  70:   local reason="$1"
  71:   echo "$reason" >&2
  72:   exit 2
  73: }
  74: 
  75: # v3.14 A・啀reToolUse permissionDecision:deny JSON 諡ｦ謌ｪ・・xit 0 + stdout JSON・・# 逕ｨ莠・Bash / mcp__ 蟾･蜈ｷ逧・guard窶披濡itHub #23284 隶ｰ蠖・Bash-tool 逧・exit-2 蝨ｨ譟蝉ｺ帷沿譛ｬ蜿ｪ謚･髞吩ｸ肴協謌ｪ・・# permissionDecision JSON 譏ｯ譁・｡｣逧・ｨｳ蛛･諡ｦ謌ｪ霍ｯ蠕・Ｇile guard 莉咲畑 block_with_reason・・xit-2 蜿ｯ髱・峨・# 驛ｨ鄂ｲ蜑埼｡ｻ live-verify・・to-doctor / 譛ｬ莨夊ｯ晏ｮ樊ｵ具ｼ会ｼ幄凶隸･迚域悽 JSON 荵滉ｸ肴協・碁蝗・block_with_reason縲・deny_with_reason() {
  76:   local reason="$1"
  77:   if [ "$HAS_JQ" = "1" ]; then
  78:     # -c 邏ｧ蜃題ｾ灘・・壻ｸ惹ｸ区婿譌-jq printf 霍ｯ蠕・ｭ苓鰍蜷悟ｽ｢・・"...":"deny"} 譌遨ｺ譬ｼ・会ｼ・    # 蜷ｦ蛻・jq 鮟倩ｮ､ pretty-print 蟶ｦ遨ｺ譬ｼ・瑚ｷｨ邇ｯ蠅・grep 譽豬倶ｼ壽ｼゑｼ・3.14 CI 螳樊ｵ具ｼ哭inux jq 霍ｯ蠕・・ 7 eval 謖ゑｼ・    jq -cn --arg r "$reason" \
  79:       '{hookSpecificOutput:{hookEventName:"PreToolUse",permissionDecision:"deny",permissionDecisionReason:$r}}'
  80:   else
  81:     # 譌 jq・・indows git-bash・会ｼ壽焔蟾･諡ｼ JSON・罫eason 霓ｬ荵・\ " 謐｢陦・    local esc
  82:     esc=$(printf '%s' "$reason" | sed 's/\\/\\\\/g; s/"/\\"/g' | tr '\n' ' ')
  83:     printf '{"hookSpecificOutput":{"hookEventName":"PreToolUse","permissionDecision":"deny","permissionDecisionReason":"%s"}}\n' "$esc"
  84:   fi
  85:   exit 0
  86: }
  87: 
  88: # 霓ｯ謠宣・・夂畑 additionalContext JSON 霎灘・・・laude 逵句芦菴・ｸ埼仆豁｢・・# 髴隕・jq・帷ｼｺ螟ｱ蛻咎剄郤ｧ荳ｺ stdout warning
  89: soft_remind() {
  90:   local context="$1"
  91:   local event="${HOOK_EVENT:-PostToolUse}"
  92:   if [ "$HAS_JQ" = "1" ]; then
  93:     jq -n --arg ctx "$context" --arg ev "$event" \
  94:       '{hookSpecificOutput: {hookEventName: $ev, additionalContext: $ctx}}'
  95:   else
  96:     # 髯咲ｺｧ・啼cho 蛻ｰ stdout・・laude 蜿ｯ閭ｽ逵句芦・御ｽ・撼扈捺桷蛹厄ｼ・    echo "[$event additionalContext]"
  97:     echo "$context"
  98:   fi
  99:   exit 0
 100: }
 101: 
 102: # 譽豬矩｡ｹ逶ｮ郤ｧ override hook
 103: maybe_run_override() {
 104:   local hook_name="$1"
 105:   local cwd="${HOOK_CWD:-.}"
 106:   local override="${cwd}/.claude/hooks-overrides/${hook_name}.sh"
 107:   if [ -f "$override" ]; then
 108:     # 謚・stdin JSON 騾丈ｼ扈・override
 109:     echo "$HOOK_JSON" | exec bash "$override"
 110:   fi
 111: }
 112: 
 113: # Audit log
 114: audit_log() {
 115:   local event="$1"
 116:   local details="${2:-}"
 117:   local cwd="${HOOK_CWD:-.}"
 118:   if [ -d "${cwd}/.claude/agent-logs" ]; then
 119:     local day=$(date +%Y-%m-%d 2>/dev/null || echo unknown)
 120:     local ts=$(date -Iseconds 2>/dev/null || date)
 121:     # 邂蜊・JSON 霓ｬ荵会ｼ壼悉謗牙曙蠑募捷
 122:     local safe_details="${details//\"/\\\"}"
 123:     printf '{"ts":"%s","hook":"%s","event":"%s","details":"%s","session":"%s"}\n' \
 124:       "$ts" "${0##*/}" "$event" "$safe_details" "${HOOK_SESSION_ID:-}" \
 125:       >> "${cwd}/.claude/agent-logs/${day}.jsonl" 2>/dev/null
 126:   fi
 127: }
 128: 
 129: # 蜈ｼ螳ｹ譌ｧ API・井ｿ晉蕗莉･蜈咲ｴ邇ｰ譛・hook・・require_jq() {
 130:   return 0  # v3.8 荳榊・萓晁ｵ・jq・梧ｻ譏ｯ OK・育畑 fallback parser・・}
 131: 
 132: # 笏笏笏 v3.13 O7・壼黒貅先ｭ｣蛻呻ｼ磯亟螟・guard 蜷・・荳莉ｽ貍らｧｻ 窶・learned rule 2026-05-12・俄楳笏笏
 133: 
 134: # forbidden 霍ｯ蠕・fallback pattern・・SOT 郛ｺ螟ｱ譌ｶ逕ｨ・峨Ｄanonical 蜚ｯ荳貅舌・# 豁､蜑・forbidden-guard / mcp-guard / codex-bridge 荳牙､・推蜀呻ｼ慶odex-bridge 驍｣莉ｽ霑倡ｼｺ
 135: # billing/keys/terraform/.github/workflows 竊・貍らｧｻ縲らｻ滉ｸ蛻ｰ霑咎㈹縲・forbidden_fallback_pattern() {
 136:   echo 'auth/|payment/|billing/|secrets/|keys/|migration|crypto/|infra/|terraform/|\.github/workflows/'
 137: }
 138: 
 139: # destructive SQL 蜈ｱ莠ｫ譬ｸ蠢・ｼ・ROP/TRUNCATE/譌 WHERE 逧・DELETE・峨・# 蜷・guard 蝨ｨ豁､譬ｸ蠢・ｸ・compose 閾ｪ蟾ｱ逧・ｸ贋ｸ区枚謇ｩ螻包ｼ・#   - destructive-action-guard・域沖 Bash 蜻ｽ莉､・会ｼ喞ore + 螟門｣ｳ蛹・｣・ｼ・sql/mongo/redis・・#   - mcp-guard・域沖 execute_sql query 蜿よ焚・会ｼ喞ore + UPDATE-no-WHERE
 140: # 蜊墓ｺ先ｸ蠢・亟荳､霎ｹ DROP/TRUNCATE 螳壻ｹ画ｼらｧｻ縲・destructive_sql_core() {
 141:   echo '\bDROP\s+(TABLE|DATABASE|SCHEMA|INDEX)\b|\bTRUNCATE\b|DELETE\s+FROM\s+[a-z_]+\s*(;|$)'
 142: }
 143: 
 144: # hook/pre-commit 扈戊ｿ・ｨ｡蠑擾ｼ・40117 6+ 遘咲ｻ戊ｿ・擇・峨Ｄanonical 蜚ｯ荳貅舌・# 豁､蜑・bypass-guard.sh・・egacy・我ｸ・engine/guards.mjs 蜷・・荳莉ｽ蟄鈴擇諡ｷ雍・竊・貍らｧｻ鬟朱勦
 145: # ・亥酔 O7 forbidden/destructive 蜊墓ｺ仙喧・峨Ｆngine/lib.mjs 逧・BYPASS_PATTERNS 蟶ｸ驥・# 蠢・｡ｻ荳取悽蜃ｽ謨ｰ霎灘・騾仙ｭ苓鰍逶ｸ遲会ｼ・val 073 譁ｭ險髞∝ｮ夲ｼ峨・bypass_patterns() {
 146:   # core.hooksPath・嘛4.4b 蜀ｳ譁ｭ 窶披・蟷ｿ荵・token・域協荳蛻・core.hooksPath 謠仙所・峨・  # 譖ｾ蟆晁ｯ輔悟宵諡ｦ蜀吶崎ｯｻ/蜀・carve-out 菫ｮ隸ｯ諡ｦ蜿ｪ隸ｻ逧・FP・・ 霓ｮ蟇ｹ謚鈴ｪ瑚ｯ・ｼ・ agent・蛾占ｽｮ蜃ｻ遨ｿ・・  #   霓ｮ1 git竊団onfig 逶ｸ驍ｻ髞夊｢ｫ git -C . 蜃ｻ遨ｿ・幄ｽｮ2 遨ｺ蠑募捷蟇ｹ core.hooksPath'' 騾・ｸ・・  #   霓ｮ3 蠑募捷蛹・桃菴懃ｬｦ蛟ｼ ")"/";"縲・{IFS} 豕ｨ蜈･縲∝渚譁懈擒扈ｭ陦後・  # 扈楢ｮｺ・嘖tatic regex 譌豕募ｮ牙・蛹ｺ蛻・core.hooksPath 逧・ｯｻ/蜀呻ｼ・hell 蠑募捷/螻募ｼ隸ｭ荵・regex 蟒ｺ讓｡荳堺ｺ・ｼ峨・  # 蟷ｿ荵・token縲梧協荳蛻・署蜿翫・ 蜚ｯ荳 adversarial-proof 逧・ｧｿ蜉ｿ・・ail-safe・会ｼ幄ｯｻ FP 譏ｯ逅・ｮｺ諤ｧ逧・  # ・域裏逵溷ｮ樊ｶ郁ｴｹ譁ｹ・單octor 逶ｴ謗･譟･ .git/hooks/pre-commit 荳崎ｵｰ git config・峨ら悄髴隸ｻ逕ｨ
 147:   # `git rev-parse --git-path hooks` 謌・CTO_BYPASS_ALLOWED=1縲りｯｦ隗・DECISIONS ADR-010縲・  # 笞・・菫晉蕗逧・悄謾ｶ逶奇ｼ域ｶ郁ｴｹ譁ｹ螂醍ｺｦ・会ｼ壼源驟榊燕蜈亥翁蠑募捷/蜿肴万譚蟄礼ｬｦ・・ypass-guard.sh SCAN_CMD tr -d /
 148:   # guards.mjs scanCmd replace・俄披・蟷ｿ荵・token + 蜑･蟄礼ｬｦ蟇ｹ core.hooks'Path' / "core.hooksPath" /
 149:   # ${IFS} 豕ｨ蜈･ / 蠑募捷謫堺ｽ懃ｬｦ蛟ｼ蜈ｨ驛ｨ蜻ｽ荳ｭ・域ｯ疲悴蜑･逧・立 pattern 荳･譬ｼ譖ｴ蠑ｺ・碁溜蜷井ｺ・立 pattern 貍冗噪蠑募捷謠貞・・峨・  echo '--no-verify|git\s+commit\s+-n($|\s)|core\.hooksPath|HUSKY=0|hooks-disable|chmod\s+-x.*husky|git\s+stash[^|]*&&[^|]*commit|SKIP=|--allow-empty\s+--dry-run|git\s+config.*hooksPath'
 150: }
---.codex/hooks/branch-guard.sh---
   1: #!/usr/bin/env bash
   2: # v4.0: Node guard engine 莨伜・・嬾ode 郛ｺ螟ｱ謌・CTO_GUARD_ENGINE=legacy 竊・荳区婿 legacy 螳樒鴫
   3: # ・・3.15 蜀ｻ扈難ｼ碁峺郤｢郤ｿ逵溽ｩｺ 窶・v3.14 verdict Phase-1 遑ｬ譚｡莉ｶ・峨ょｼ墓梼・啼ngine/guard.mjs
   4: GUARD_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
   5: if [ "${CTO_GUARD_ENGINE:-engine}" != "legacy" ] && command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1 && [ -f "$GUARD_DIR/engine/guard.mjs" ]; then
   6:   exec node "$GUARD_DIR/engine/guard.mjs" branch-guard
   7: fi
   8: # 笊絶武 legacy fallback・・3.15 蜴溷ｮ樒鴫・悟・扈謎ｸ榊・貍碑ｿ幢ｼ俄武笊・# 體∝ｾ・#8・壼・蛻帛ｻｺ Git 蛻・髪蜀榊勘謇・窶・PreToolUse(Edit|Write|MultiEdit)
   9: # main / master branch 荳顔峩謗･ Edit 竊・exit 2 髦ｻ豁｢
  10: # Opt-out: CTO_MAIN_EDIT_ALLOWED=1・井ｻ・hotfix 邏ｧ諤･蝨ｺ譎ｯ・・#
  11: # 笞・・蟾ｲ遏･隸ｭ荵牙ｷｮ・・*隶ｾ隶｡螯よｭ､・碁撼 bug** 窶・v4.7 螳樊ｵ区ｾ・ｸ・ｼ悟響隸ｯ蛻､荳ｺ legacy 貍乗協・会ｼ・#   譛ｬ螻ょ宵諡ｦ Edit/Write/MultiEdit・・3.15 蜀ｻ扈馴擇・峨・*Bash 螻ら噪 `git commit/merge/push` 諡ｦ謌ｪ
  12: #   譏ｯ v4.0c 譁ｰ蠅櫁ｯｭ荵会ｼ御ｻ・惠 engine 螳樒鴫**・・ngine/guards.mjs branchGuardBash・峨・#   謨・node 郛ｺ螟ｱ / CTO_GUARD_ENGINE=legacy 譌ｶ・形git commit` 邀ｻ蜻ｽ莉､荳崎｢ｫ譛ｬ螻よ協 窶披・霑呎弍
  13: #   縲悟ｺ疲･髯咲ｺｧ菫昜ｽ乗ｸ蠢・Edit/Write 郤｢郤ｿ縲咲噪譛画э蜿冶・・御ｸ肴弍 false-negative縲・#   v4.7 逧・ｷｨ莉・cd / git -C / 蜷御ｸｲ checkout -b 諢溽衍蜷檎炊・憩ngine-only縲・set -uo pipefail
  14: SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
  15: source "$SCRIPT_DIR/lib/common.sh"
  16: 
  17: require_jq || exit 0
  18: read_hook_input
  19: maybe_run_override "branch-guard"
  20: 
  21: # v4.0e・・odex ﾂｧ48・会ｼ夊ｷｯ蠕・ｽ剃ｸ 窶・荳・engine canonPath 蜷梧ｬｾ・・ackslash竊・縲｀SYS(/c/)竊貞次逕溘∝悉蟆ｾ譁懈擒縲・# Windows 螟ｧ蟆丞・荳肴撫諢滂ｼ峨ゆｿ・engine/legacy parity縲・_canon() {
  22:   local p="${1//\\//}"
  23:   local win=0
  24:   case "$(uname -s 2>/dev/null)" in MINGW*|MSYS*|CYGWIN*) win=1 ;; esac
  25:   if [ "$win" = "1" ]; then
  26:     case "$p" in
  27:       /[A-Za-z]/*|/[A-Za-z]) local d="${p:1:1}"; p="${d}:${p:2}" ;;  # /c/foo 竊・c:/foo
  28:     esac
  29:     p="${p,,}"  # Windows FS 螟ｧ蟆丞・荳肴撫諢・  fi
  30:   while [ "$p" != "${p%/}" ]; do p="${p%/}"; done  # 蜑･蜈ｨ驛ｨ蟆ｾ譁懈擒・亥ｯｹ鮨・engine .replace(/\/+$/,'')・・  printf '%s' "$p"
  31: }
  32: 
  33: # 莉・ｯｹ file 邀ｻ蟾･蜈ｷ逕滓譜
  34: [ -z "$HOOK_FILE_PATH" ] && exit 0
  35: 
  36: # 譽豬句ｽ灘燕 git branch
  37: cd "${HOOK_CWD:-.}" 2>/dev/null
  38: BRANCH=$(git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD 2>/dev/null)
  39: [ -z "$BRANCH" ] && exit 0  # 荳榊惠 git repo 蜀・ｼ瑚ｷｳ霑・
  40: # 蜊ｱ髯ｩ branch 蜷榊黒
  41: case "$BRANCH" in
  42:   main|master|production|prod|release)
  43:     # v4.0e・井ｿｮ 2026-07-02 隸ｯ諡ｦ + codex ﾂｧ48 蜉蝗ｺﾃ・・会ｼ壻ｻ・協蠖灘燕 git 蟾･菴懈大・譁・ｻｶ 窶・    # 菫晄侃蛻・髪荳雁・莉灘ｺ灘､匁枚莉ｶ・亥ｦ・~/.claude/.../memory/*.md・我ｸ取悽莉・main 譌蜈ｳ 竊・謾ｾ陦後・    # 蟾･菴懈第ｹ = 莉・cwd 謖・git cdup 逶ｸ蟇ｹ荳顔握・磯撼 --show-toplevel 逧・real 霍ｯ蠕・竊・symlink 蛻ｫ蜷堺ｸ肴ｼ乗協・峨・    # 荳・engine fileInsideWorktree() 逧・cdup 荳顔握 + canon parity縲・    _NF="${HOOK_FILE_PATH//\\//}"
  44:     _INSIDE=1
  45:     case "$_NF" in
  46:       /*|[A-Za-z]:/*)  # 扈晏ｯｹ霍ｯ蠕・竊・髴關ｽ蝨ｨ蟾･菴懈第ｹ蜑咲ｼ蜀・燕邂嶺ｻ灘ｺ灘・
  47:         _CDUP=$(git rev-parse --show-cdup 2>/dev/null)  # 蟾ｲ蝨ｨ cwd 蜀・ｼ井ｸ頑婿 cd・会ｼ帶ｹ逶ｮ蠖・遨ｺ
  48:         _ROOT="${HOOK_CWD:-.}"; _ROOT="${_ROOT//\\//}"
  49:         while [ "$_ROOT" != "${_ROOT%/}" ]; do _ROOT="${_ROOT%/}"; done  # 蜑･蜈ｨ驛ｨ蟆ｾ譁懈擒蜀堺ｸ顔握・磯亟隸ｯ蜷・'..' 螻らｺｧ・悟ｯｹ鮨・engine・・        _t="$_CDUP"
  50:         while [ -n "$_t" ]; do  # 豈丈ｸｪ '../' 荳顔握荳螻ゑｼ亥●逡吝惠 cwd 遨ｺ髣ｴ・・          case "$_t" in
  51:             ../*) _ROOT="${_ROOT%/*}"; _t="${_t#../}" ;;
  52:             *) _t="" ;;
  53:           esac
  54:         done
  55:         _CF=$(_canon "$_NF"); _CB=$(_canon "$_ROOT")
  56:         _INSIDE=0
  57:         case "$_CF" in
  58:           "$_CB"|"$_CB"/*) _INSIDE=1 ;;
  59:         esac
  60:         ;;
  61:     esac
  62:     if [ "$_INSIDE" = "0" ]; then
  63:       audit_log "main-edit-outside-repo-allowed" "branch=$BRANCH file=$HOOK_FILE_PATH"
  64:       exit 0
  65:     fi
  66: 
  67:     if [ "${CTO_MAIN_EDIT_ALLOWED:-0}" = "1" ]; then
  68:       audit_log "main-edit-allowed-emergency" "branch=$BRANCH file=$HOOK_FILE_PATH"
  69:       exit 0
  70:     fi
  71: 
  72:     audit_log "main-edit-blocked" "branch=$BRANCH file=$HOOK_FILE_PATH"
  73: 
  74:     block_with_reason "尅 體∝ｾ・#8 BLOCKED: 蠖灘燕蝨ｨ \`$BRANCH\` 蛻・髪荳顔峩謗･ Edit
  75: 
  76: 譁・ｻｶ・・HOOK_FILE_PATH
  77: 蛻・髪・・BRANCH ・亥女菫晄侃・・
  78: 逶ｴ謗･郛冶ｾ台ｸｻ蛻・髪霑晏渚 #8・亥・蛻帛ｻｺ Git 蛻・髪蜀榊勘謇具ｼ峨・
  79: 豁｣遑ｮ蛛壽ｳ包ｼ・  git checkout -b feat/<short-name>
  80:   # 謌門渕莠主ｷｲ譛・PR 逧・・謾ｯ・・  git checkout -b fix/<issue-short>
  81: 
  82: 邏ｧ諤･ hotfix 萓句､厄ｼ井ｻ・惠莠区腐蜩榊ｺ比ｸｭ菴ｿ逕ｨ・会ｼ・  export CTO_MAIN_EDIT_ALLOWED=1   # 蜊墓ｬ｡莨夊ｯ・+ audit log 豌ｸ荵・ｮｰ蠖・
  83: 蜿り・ｼ咾LAUDE.md 體∝ｾ・#8"
  84:     ;;
  85: esac
  86: 
  87: exit 0
---.codex/hooks/destructive-action-guard.sh---
   1: #!/usr/bin/env bash
   2: # v4.0: Node guard engine 莨伜・・嬾ode 郛ｺ螟ｱ謌・CTO_GUARD_ENGINE=legacy 竊・荳区婿 legacy 螳樒鴫
   3: # ・・3.15 蜀ｻ扈難ｼ碁峺郤｢郤ｿ逵溽ｩｺ 窶・v3.14 verdict Phase-1 遑ｬ譚｡莉ｶ・峨ょｼ墓梼・啼ngine/guard.mjs
   4: GUARD_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
   5: if [ "${CTO_GUARD_ENGINE:-engine}" != "legacy" ] && command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1 && [ -f "$GUARD_DIR/engine/guard.mjs" ]; then
   6:   exec node "$GUARD_DIR/engine/guard.mjs" destructive-action-guard
   7: fi
   8: # 笊絶武 legacy fallback・・3.15 蜴溷ｮ樒鴫・悟・扈謎ｸ榊・貍碑ｿ幢ｼ俄武笊・# v3.10.1 郤｢郤ｿ螻ゑｼ單estructive action gate
   9: # OWASP Agentic Top 10 2026 窶・ASI01 (Agent Goal Hijacking) 螟ｴ蜿ｷ鬟朱勦
  10: # 謨呵ｮｭ・啀ocketOS 2026-04-25 窶・Cursor+Claude Opus 4.6 agent 9 遘貞唖逕滉ｺｧ蠎・+ 蜈ｨ驛ｨ螟・ｻｽ
  11: #       (https://www.theregister.com/2026/04/27/cursoropus_agent_snuffs_out_pocketos/)
  12: # 譬ｹ蝗・嗤verprivileged token + 蜈ｱ莠ｫ backup volume + 郛ｺ destructive-action gate
  13: #
  14: # 諡ｦ謌ｪ・壻ｻｻ菴穂ｸ榊庄騾・destructive 蜻ｽ莉､・亥唖蠎・/ drop / rm -rf 驥崎ｦ∫岼蠖・/ 謦､髞譛榊苅 etc・・set -uo pipefail
  15: SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
  16: source "$SCRIPT_DIR/lib/common.sh"
  17: 
  18: read_hook_input
  19: maybe_run_override "destructive-action-guard"
  20: 
  21: # 莉・ｯｹ Bash 蟾･蜈ｷ逕滓譜
  22: [ "$HOOK_TOOL_NAME" != "Bash" ] && exit 0
  23: [ -z "$HOOK_BASH_CMD" ] && exit 0
  24: 
  25: # v3.11 fix・磯｣櫁ｽｮ隨ｬ 8 霓ｮ 窶・architect-critic 蜿醍鴫 v3.10.2 蠑募・螳牙・蝗槫ｽ抵ｼ会ｼ・# v3.10.2 謨ｴ谿ｵ蜑･遖ｻ蠑募捷蜀・ｮｹ 竊・psql -c "DROP DATABASE" / rm -rf "$HOME" 騾・ｸ・・alse NEGATIVE・悟ｮ牙・蝗槫ｽ抵ｼ・# 菫ｮ・壼宵蜑･ heredoc body・亥・譁・｡｣荳ｻ蝨ｺ譎ｯ・会ｼ帛ｼ募捷蜀・ｮｹ**菫晉蕗譽豬・*・亥多莉､蜿よ焚莨壽鴬陦鯉ｼ峨・# 郤ｯ霎灘・蝨ｺ譎ｯ・・cho/printf 蠑螟ｴ + 譌 shell 謫堺ｽ懃ｬｦ・画燕謨ｴ菴捺叛陦・窶・蜈ｼ鬘ｾ false positive 荳主ｮ牙・縲・SCAN_CMD=$(printf '%s' "$HOOK_BASH_CMD" | sed -E "s/<<-?'?[A-Za-z_]+'?.*//")
  26: 
  27: # 郤ｯ echo/printf 霎灘・・域裏 && || ; | $() 謫堺ｽ懃ｬｦ・俄・ 蜀・ｮｹ譏ｯ扈吩ｺｺ逵狗噪譁・悽・碁撼謇ｧ陦・竊・謾ｾ陦・if echo "$SCAN_CMD" | grep -qE '^[[:space:]]*(echo|printf)[[:space:]]' \
  28:    && ! echo "$SCAN_CMD" | grep -qE '&&|\|\||;|\$\(|\|[[:space:]]'; then
  29:   exit 0
  30: fi
  31: 
  32: # Destructive 讓｡蠑丞・陦ｨ・井ｿ晏ｮ・窶・螳∬ｯｯ諡ｦ荵滉ｸ肴ｼ擾ｼ・# 蛻・3 邀ｻ・・#   A. 譁・ｻｶ邉ｻ扈溽ｺｧ轣ｾ髫ｾ・嗷m -rf / / rm -rf ~ / find -delete
  33: #   B. 謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎鍋ｺｧ轣ｾ髫ｾ・咼ROP TABLE / DROP DATABASE / TRUNCATE / DELETE FROM (譌 WHERE)
  34: #   C. 莠第恪蜉｡/蟷ｳ蜿ｰ郤ｧ・嗾erraform destroy / vercel rm / railway destroy / supabase project delete / aws s3 rb / gh repo delete
  35: 
  36: # A. 譁・ｻｶ邉ｻ扈滂ｼ・3.11: 霍ｯ蠕・燕蜉 ["']? 螳ｹ蠢榊ｼ募捷蛹・｣ｹ・悟屏 v3.11 荳榊・蜑･蠑募捷・・FS_PATTERNS='rm\s+-rf\s+["'"'"']?/($|\s|["'"'"'])|rm\s+-rf\s+["'"'"']?~($|\s|["'"'"'])|rm\s+-rf\s+["'"'"']?[$]HOME|rm\s+-rf\s+["'"'"']?\.\s|rm\s+-rf\s+["'"'"']?\*($|\s)|find\s+/?\s.*-delete|>\s*/dev/sda|mkfs|dd\s+if=.*of=/dev/'
  37: 
  38: # B. 謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎難ｼ・3.13 O7・售QL 譬ｸ蠢・ｻ・common.sh 蜊墓ｺ・+ 譛ｬ guard 逧・shell 螟門｣ｳ謇ｩ螻包ｼ・DB_PATTERNS="$(destructive_sql_core)|psql.*-c.*DROP|mongo.*dropDatabase|redis-cli.*FLUSHALL"
  39: 
  40: # C. 莠第恪蜉｡ destructive・・3.11: 蜈ｳ髞ｮ襍・ｺ仙燕蜉 ["']? 螳ｹ蠢榊ｼ募捷・・CLOUD_PATTERNS='terraform\s+destroy|vercel\s+rm\s.*--yes|railway\s+(down|destroy)|supabase\s+project\s+delete|aws\s+s3\s+rb\s+["'"'"']?s3://.*--force|aws\s+rds\s+delete-db-instance|aws\s+ec2\s+terminate-instances.*--force|gh\s+repo\s+delete|gh\s+secret\s+remove|firebase\s+(use\s+.*&&.*deploy|projects:delete)|heroku\s+apps:destroy|fly\s+apps\s+destroy|kubectl\s+delete\s+(ns|namespace|cluster|all)|docker\s+system\s+prune\s+--all\s+--volumes'
  41: 
  42: # 螟榊粋 destructive・井ｸ榊庄騾・+ 螟ｧ隗・ｨ｡・・COMBINED_DESTRUCTIVE="${FS_PATTERNS}|${DB_PATTERNS}|${CLOUD_PATTERNS}"
  43: 
  44: if echo "$SCAN_CMD" | grep -qiE -- "$COMBINED_DESTRUCTIVE"; then
  45:   # Opt-out: 譫∫ｫｯ諠・・・亥ｦら悄隕∵ｸ・炊豬玖ｯ慕識蠅・ｼ蛾怙 explicit 隗｣髞・  if [ "${CTO_DESTRUCTIVE_CONFIRMED:-0}" = "1" ]; then
  46:     audit_log "destructive-action-allowed" "cmd=$(echo "$HOOK_BASH_CMD" | head -c 200) env=1"
  47:     exit 0
  48:   fi
  49: 
  50:   audit_log "destructive-action-blocked" "cmd=$(echo "$HOOK_BASH_CMD" | head -c 200)"
  51: 
  52:   deny_with_reason "尅 v3.10.1 DESTRUCTIVE ACTION BLOCKED
  53: 
  54: 蜻ｽ莉､・喀`$(echo "$HOOK_BASH_CMD" | head -c 300)\`
  55: 
  56: 蜻ｽ荳ｭ荳榊庄騾・destructive 讓｡蠑擾ｼ・m -rf / DROP TABLE / terraform destroy / etc・峨・
  57: 蜿り・ｼ・- OWASP Agentic Top 10 (2026) ASI01: Agent Goal Hijacking 窶・螟ｴ蜿ｷ鬟朱勦
  58: - PocketOS 9 遘堤∪髫ｾ・・026-04-25・・ Cursor+Claude 蛻逕滉ｺｧ蠎・+ 螟・ｻｽ
  59:   https://www.theregister.com/2026/04/27/cursoropus_agent_snuffs_out_pocketos/
  60: 
  61: 豁｣遑ｮ蛛壽ｳ包ｼ・  1. 蜈育畑 \`echo\` 謌・\`--dry-run\` 讓｡諡滉ｸ驕咲恚蠖ｱ蜩崎激蝗ｴ
  62:   2. 螯ら函莠ｧ邇ｯ蠅・竊・隶ｩ莠ｺ螳｡ + 襍ｰ spec-driven
  63:   3. 螯よｵ玖ｯ・/ 荳ｴ譌ｶ邇ｯ蠅・竊・逕ｨ譖ｴ邊ｾ遑ｮ逧・多莉､・磯∩蜈・-rf / / -rf \$HOME 遲臥∪髫ｾ諤ｧ蟷ｿ蠎ｦ・・  4. 謨ｰ謐ｮ蠎捺桃菴懷ｿ・｡ｻ蜷ｫ WHERE / LIMIT
  64: 
  65: 邏ｧ諤･遑ｮ隶､・井ｻ・in-test-env 荳泌ｷｲ螟・ｻｽ・会ｼ・  export CTO_DESTRUCTIVE_CONFIRMED=1   # 蜊墓ｬ｡莨夊ｯ・+ audit 豌ｸ荵・ｮｰ蠖・  # 辟ｶ蜷朱㍾霍題ｯ･蜻ｽ莉､"
  66: fi
  67:

---.codex/hooks/bypass-guard.sh---
   1: #!/usr/bin/env bash
   2: # v4.0: Node guard engine 莨伜・・嬾ode 郛ｺ螟ｱ謌・CTO_GUARD_ENGINE=legacy 竊・荳区婿 legacy 螳樒鴫
   3: # ・・3.15 蜀ｻ扈難ｼ碁峺郤｢郤ｿ逵溽ｩｺ 窶・v3.14 verdict Phase-1 遑ｬ譚｡莉ｶ・峨ょｼ墓梼・啼ngine/guard.mjs
   4: GUARD_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
   5: if [ "${CTO_GUARD_ENGINE:-engine}" != "legacy" ] && command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1 && [ -f "$GUARD_DIR/engine/guard.mjs" ]; then
   6:   exec node "$GUARD_DIR/engine/guard.mjs" bypass-guard
   7: fi
   8: # 笊絶武 legacy fallback・・3.15 蜴溷ｮ樒鴫・悟・扈謎ｸ榊・貍碑ｿ幢ｼ俄武笊・# 髦ｲ #40117 螟夂ｭ也払扈戊ｿ・窶・PreToolUse(Bash)
   9: # Anthropic 閾ｪ螳ｶ issue 譏ｾ遉ｺ Claude 莨夂畑 6+ 遘肴婿蠑冗ｻ戊ｿ・pre-commit hook
  10: # 霑吩ｸｪ guard 諡ｦ謌ｪ謇譛牙ｷｲ遏･ bypass 讓｡蠑擾ｼ憩xit 2 + stderr 蝟ょ屓 Claude
  11: set -uo pipefail
  12: SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
  13: source "$SCRIPT_DIR/lib/common.sh"
  14: 
  15: require_jq || exit 0
  16: read_hook_input
  17: maybe_run_override "bypass-guard"
  18: 
  19: # 莉・ｯｹ Bash 蟾･蜈ｷ逕滓譜
  20: [ "$HOOK_TOOL_NAME" != "Bash" ] && exit 0
  21: [ -z "$HOOK_BASH_CMD" ] && exit 0
  22: 
  23: # 6+ 遘・bypass 讓｡蠑擾ｼ亥渕莠・#40117 螳樊・隗ょｯ・+ Anthropic discussion・・# - --no-verify / -n: 霍ｳ霑・git hook
  24: # - core.hooksPath: 驥榊・ hook 霍ｯ蠕・# - HUSKY=0 / hooks-disable: 遖・husky
  25: # - chmod -x .husky: 蛻 hook 謇ｧ陦梧揀
  26: # - git stash + commit + pop: 蛟・stash 扈戊ｿ・# - SKIP / skip: 荳莠帛ｷ･蜈ｷ逧・bypass env
  27: # 蜊墓ｺ撰ｼ嗔attern 逕ｱ common.sh bypass_patterns() 謠蝉ｾ幢ｼ磯亟 legacy/engine 貍らｧｻ・薫7・・BYPASS_PATTERNS="$(bypass_patterns)"
  28: 
  29: # v4.4b 蠑募捷謠貞・騾・ｸ遑ｬ蛹厄ｼ壼翁蠑募捷/蜿肴万譚縲悟ｭ礼ｬｦ縲榊錘蛹ｹ驟搾ｼ井ｸ・engine guards.mjs scanCmd 騾仙ｭ苓鰍蜷梧ｭ･・峨・# shell 謇ｧ陦悟燕莨壼帥謗芽ｿ吩ｺ帛ｭ礼ｬｦ 窶披・core.hooks'Path' / "core.hooksPath" / core\.hooksPath 蠖剃ｸ蜷取燕蜿ｯ蜻ｽ荳ｭ縲・# 蟷ｿ荵・core.hooksPath token + 譛ｬ蜑･蟄礼ｬｦ = 蟇ｹ 3 霓ｮ蟇ｹ謚鈴ｪ瑚ｯ∫噪蠑募捷/扈ｭ陦碁・ｸ蜈咲稔縲ょ宵蛻荳榊｢・竊・荳･譬ｼ雜・寔縲・# \047=' \042=" \134=\
  30: SCAN_CMD=$(printf '%s' "$HOOK_BASH_CMD" | tr -d '\047\042\134')
  31: 
  32: if echo "$SCAN_CMD" | grep -qE -- "$BYPASS_PATTERNS"; then
  33:   # Opt-out: 邏ｧ諤･諠・・荳区焔蜉ｨ隶ｾ CTO_BYPASS_ALLOWED=1
  34:   if [ "${CTO_BYPASS_ALLOWED:-0}" = "1" ]; then
  35:     audit_log "bypass-allowed-emergency" "cmd=$HOOK_BASH_CMD"
  36:     exit 0
  37:   fi
  38: 
  39:   audit_log "bypass-blocked" "cmd=$HOOK_BASH_CMD"
  40: 
  41:   deny_with_reason "尅 BLOCKED: 譽豬句芦 hook/pre-commit 扈戊ｿ・ｰ晁ｯ・
  42: 蜻ｽ莉､・喀`$HOOK_BASH_CMD\`
  43: 
  44: 霑吝多荳ｭ #40117 邀ｻ螟夂ｭ也払扈戊ｿ・ｨ｡蠑擾ｼ・-no-verify / hooksPath 驥榊・ / stash 扈戊ｿ・/ chmod 遲会ｼ峨・Anthropic 閾ｪ蟾ｱ逧・issue #40117 隸∵・・咾LAUDE.md 譁・悽隗・・荳榊､滂ｼ悟ｿ・｡ｻ hook 蠑ｺ蛻ｶ縲・
  45: 豁｣遑ｮ蛛壽ｳ包ｼ井ｿｮ蝗荳堺ｿｮ髞・ｼ会ｼ・  1. 謚雁､ｱ雍･逧・hook 霎灘・雍ｴ蜃ｺ譚･
  46:   2. 謌台ｻｬ荳襍ｷ隸頑妙荳ｺ莉荵・hook 螟ｱ雍･
  47:   3. 菫ｮ螟肴ｹ譛ｬ蜴溷屏・瑚ｮｩ hook 閾ｪ辟ｶ騾夊ｿ・
  48: 邏ｧ諤･萓句､厄ｼ井ｻ・惠逕滉ｺｧ莠区腐譌ｶ・会ｼ・  export CTO_BYPASS_ALLOWED=1   # 蜊墓ｬ｡莨夊ｯ・+ audit log 豌ｸ荵・ｮｰ蠖・
  49: 蜿り・ｼ喇ttps://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/40117"
  50: fi
  51: 
  52: exit 0
---.codex/hooks/forbidden-guard.sh---
   1: #!/usr/bin/env bash
   2: # v4.0: Node guard engine 莨伜・・嬾ode 郛ｺ螟ｱ謌・CTO_GUARD_ENGINE=legacy 竊・荳区婿 legacy 螳樒鴫
   3: # ・・3.15 蜀ｻ扈難ｼ碁峺郤｢郤ｿ逵溽ｩｺ 窶・v3.14 verdict Phase-1 遑ｬ譚｡莉ｶ・峨ょｼ墓梼・啼ngine/guard.mjs
   4: GUARD_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
   5: if [ "${CTO_GUARD_ENGINE:-engine}" != "legacy" ] && command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1 && [ -f "$GUARD_DIR/engine/guard.mjs" ]; then
   6:   exec node "$GUARD_DIR/engine/guard.mjs" forbidden-guard
   7: fi
   8: # 笊絶武 legacy fallback・・3.15 蜴溷ｮ樒鴫・悟・扈謎ｸ榊・貍碑ｿ幢ｼ俄武笊・# ﾂｧ32.1 Forbidden 霍ｯ蠕・｡ｬ諡ｦ謌ｪ 窶・PreToolUse(Edit|Write|MultiEdit)
   9: # 隗ｦ蜿・auth/payment/secrets/migration/crypto/infra 遲芽ｷｯ蠕・竊・exit 2 髦ｻ豁｢
  10: # Opt-out: CTO_DOUBLE_SIGNED=1・磯怙蜿檎ｭｾ + spec-driven 蜷主黒谺｡隗｣髞・ｼ・set -uo pipefail
  11: SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
  12: source "$SCRIPT_DIR/lib/common.sh"
  13: 
  14: require_jq || exit 0
  15: read_hook_input
  16: maybe_run_override "forbidden-guard"
  17: 
  18: # 莉・ｯｹ file 邀ｻ蟾･蜈ｷ逕滓譜
  19: [ -z "$HOOK_FILE_PATH" ] && exit 0
  20: 
  21: # v3.9.2 fix・磯｣櫁ｽｮ莠梧ｬ｡螳樊・蜿醍鴫・会ｼ啗indows 蜿肴万譚霍ｯ蠕・翁遖ｻ髱咎ｻ伜､ｱ謨茨ｼ亥酔 immutable-guard 荵句燕逧・bug・・# normalize 蜿肴万譚 竊・豁｣譁懈擒・瑚ｮｩ grep 讓｡蠑擾ｼ育畑 /・芽・豁｣遑ｮ蛹ｹ驟・NORMALIZED_FILE="${HOOK_FILE_PATH//\\/\/}"
  22: NORMALIZED_CWD="${HOOK_CWD//\\/\/}"
  23: 
  24: # 霓ｬ鬘ｹ逶ｮ逶ｸ蟇ｹ霍ｯ蠕・ｼ亥悉謗・cwd 蜑咲ｼ莉･萓ｿ grep・・REL_PATH="${NORMALIZED_FILE#${NORMALIZED_CWD}/}"
  25: # 螯よ棡蜑･遖ｻ螟ｱ雍･・井ｸ榊惠 cwd 蜀・ｼ俄・ 逕ｨ normalized 扈晏ｯｹ霍ｯ蠕・ｮｩ grep 讓｡蠑丞源驟・[ "$REL_PATH" = "$NORMALIZED_FILE" ] && REL_PATH="$NORMALIZED_FILE"
  26: 
  27: # SSOT: scripts/forbidden-paths.txt・・3.6.1 蟾ｲ關ｽ蝨ｰ・・SSOT="${NORMALIZED_CWD}/scripts/forbidden-paths.txt"
  28: if [ ! -f "$SSOT" ]; then
  29:   # SSOT 郛ｺ螟ｱ・喃allback・・3.13 O7・壼黒貅・common.sh・悟酔謇句・ ﾂｧ32.1・・  PATTERN="$(forbidden_fallback_pattern)"
  30: else
  31:   PATTERN=$(grep -vE '^\s*(#|$)' "$SSOT" | tr '\n' '|' | sed 's/|$//')
  32: fi
  33: 
  34: [ -z "$PATTERN" ] && exit 0
  35: 
  36: # 蜻ｽ荳ｭ forbidden 霍ｯ蠕・ｼ・if echo "$REL_PATH" | grep -qE -- "($PATTERN)"; then
  37:   # Opt-out・夂畑謌ｷ蟾ｲ襍ｰ spec-driven + 蜿檎ｭｾ蜷主庄荳ｴ譌ｶ隗｣髞・  if [ "${CTO_DOUBLE_SIGNED:-0}" = "1" ]; then
  38:     audit_log "forbidden-allowed" "path=$REL_PATH double_signed=true"
  39:     exit 0
  40:   fi
  41: 
  42:   audit_log "forbidden-blocked" "path=$REL_PATH"
  43: 
  44:   block_with_reason "尅 ﾂｧ32.1 BLOCKED: \`$REL_PATH\` 蜻ｽ荳ｭ forbidden 霍ｯ蠕・
  45: 豁､霍ｯ蠕・ｦ∵ｭ｢ vibe coding・磯刀蠕・#13・会ｼ悟ｿ・｡ｻ襍ｰ spec-driven・・  1. /cto-spec specify 窶・蜈亥・ SPEC 蟷ｶ扈丈ｺｺ螳｡
  46:   2. 蜿檎ｭｾ・咾TO + 隨ｬ莠梧ｨ｡蝙狗峡遶句ｮ｡・・cto-review --cross・・  3. PR 謇・\`requires-double-review\` 譬・ｭｾ
  47: 
  48: 隸ｦ隗・.claude/rules/forbidden-paths.md・・andbook ﾂｧ32.1 / ﾂｧ19 / 體∝ｾ・#13・・邏ｧ諤･ opt-out・亥ｷｲ闔ｷ蜿檎ｭｾ蜷趣ｼ会ｼ啼xport CTO_DOUBLE_SIGNED=1   # 莉・悽莨夊ｯ晄怏謨・
  49: fi
  50: 
  51: exit 0
---.codex/hooks/immutable-guard.sh---
   1: #!/usr/bin/env bash
   2: # v4.0: Node guard engine 莨伜・・嬾ode 郛ｺ螟ｱ謌・CTO_GUARD_ENGINE=legacy 竊・荳区婿 legacy 螳樒鴫
   3: # ・・3.15 蜀ｻ扈難ｼ碁峺郤｢郤ｿ逵溽ｩｺ 窶・v3.14 verdict Phase-1 遑ｬ譚｡莉ｶ・峨ょｼ墓梼・啼ngine/guard.mjs
   4: GUARD_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
   5: if [ "${CTO_GUARD_ENGINE:-engine}" != "legacy" ] && command -v node >/dev/null 2>&1 && [ -f "$GUARD_DIR/engine/guard.mjs" ]; then
   6:   exec node "$GUARD_DIR/engine/guard.mjs" immutable-guard
   7: fi
   8: # 笊絶武 legacy fallback・・3.15 蜴溷ｮ樒鴫・悟・扈謎ｸ榊・貍碑ｿ幢ｼ俄武笊・# v3.9 郤｢郤ｿ螻ゑｼ壽協謌ｪ AI 謾ｹ Constitution / 14 體∝ｾ・/ SSOT
   9: # OWASP Agentic Top 10 (2025-12) Rogue Agent + AIVSS v0.8 self-modification = risk amplifier
  10: # Anthropic Constitutional AI: constitution 荳榊庄螯･蜊・# 蜈ｱ隸・ｼ壽園譛牙膚荳・agent 驛ｽ謚雁ｭｦ蛻ｰ逧・・霑帶仞蠑・markdown・檎ｻ昜ｸ肴隼 system prompt
  11: set -uo pipefail
  12: SCRIPT_DIR="$(cd "$(dirname "${BASH_SOURCE[0]}")" && pwd)"
  13: source "$SCRIPT_DIR/lib/common.sh"
  14: 
  15: read_hook_input
  16: maybe_run_override "immutable-guard"
  17: 
  18: [ -z "$HOOK_FILE_PATH" ] && exit 0
  19: 
  20: CWD="${HOOK_CWD:-.}"
  21: 
  22: # v3.9.3 fix・磯｣櫁ｽｮ隨ｬ 3 霓ｮ螳樊・蜿醍鴫・詣rist-fc 驛ｨ鄂ｲ譌ｶ陲ｫ閾ｪ蟾ｱ諡ｦ・会ｼ・# 蟄宣｡ｹ逶ｮ・井ｸ肴弍 ai-playbook 閾ｪ霄ｫ莉灘ｺ難ｼ牙ｺ碑ｯ･荳榊ｮ・CLAUDE.md / handbook ﾂｧ32-ﾂｧ35
  23: # 蝗荳ｺ蟄宣｡ｹ逶ｮ逧・CLAUDE.md 譏ｯ鬘ｹ逶ｮ郤ｧ驟咲ｽｮ・御ｸ肴弍 ai-playbook 14 體∝ｾ区悽霄ｫ
  24: # 閾ｪ蜉ｨ譽豬具ｼ啾i-playbook 閾ｪ霄ｫ莉灘ｺ鍋音蠕・= 蜷ｫ playbook/handbook.md・亥・莉夜｡ｹ逶ｮ reference it 菴・ｸ榊性・・IS_AI_PLAYBOOK_SELF=0
  25: if [ -f "${CWD}/playbook/handbook.md" ] && [ -d "${CWD}/playbook" ]; then
  26:   # 霑帑ｸ豁･遑ｮ隶､・喇andbook.md 蜷ｫ ﾂｧ50・・3.9 遶闃ゑｼ・  if head -200 "${CWD}/playbook/handbook.md" 2>/dev/null | grep -q "## 50\." || \
  27:      grep -q "^## 50\." "${CWD}/playbook/handbook.md" 2>/dev/null; then
  28:     IS_AI_PLAYBOOK_SELF=1
  29:   elif [ -f "${CWD}/CTO-PLAYBOOK.md" ]; then
  30:     # ai-playbook 閾ｪ霄ｫ莉灘ｺ鍋噪蜿ｦ荳荳ｪ迚ｹ蠕・    IS_AI_PLAYBOOK_SELF=1
  31:   fi
  32: fi
  33: # 逕ｨ謌ｷ蜿ｯ蠑ｺ蛻ｶ隕・尠・育識蠅・序驥丈ｼ伜・・・[ "${CTO_IS_SUBPROJECT:-0}" = "1" ] && IS_AI_PLAYBOOK_SELF=0
  34: [ "${CTO_IS_AI_PLAYBOOK_SELF:-0}" = "1" ] && IS_AI_PLAYBOOK_SELF=1
  35: 
  36: # v3.9.1 fix・・attern-detector 鬟櫁ｽｮ蜿醍鴫・会ｼ啗indows 蜿肴万譚霍ｯ蠕・+ Edit 蟾･蜈ｷ莨扈晏ｯｹ霍ｯ蠕・# 譌ｧ騾ｻ霎・${HOOK_FILE_PATH#$CWD/} 蝨ｨ蜿肴万譚霍ｯ蠕・ｸ倶ｸ榊翁遖ｻ 竊・REL 莉肴弍扈晏ｯｹ霍ｯ蠕・竊・謇譛臥ｺ｢郤ｿ NO
  37: # 菫ｮ・嗜ormalize 霍ｯ蠕・ｼ亥渚譁懈擒 竊・豁｣譁懈擒 + 蜿也嶌蟇ｹ霍ｯ蠕・+ basename 蜈懷ｺ包ｼ・NORMALIZED_FILE="${HOOK_FILE_PATH//\\/\/}"
  38: NORMALIZED_CWD="${CWD//\\/\/}"
  39: # 蜈域潔 normalized 霍ｯ蠕・翁遖ｻ蜑咲ｼ
  40: REL="${NORMALIZED_FILE#${NORMALIZED_CWD}/}"
  41: # 螯よ棡霑俶弍扈晏ｯｹ霍ｯ蠕・ｼ亥翁遖ｻ螟ｱ雍･・会ｼ檎畑 basename 蠖・REL・育ｺ｢郤ｿ蛻､譁ｭ莉・恚譁・ｻｶ蜷搾ｼ・case "$REL" in
  42:   /*|[A-Za-z]:/*)
  43:     REL=$(basename "$NORMALIZED_FILE")
  44:     ;;
  45: esac
  46: # 蜷梧慮菫晉蕗 basename 萓帷ｺ｢郤ｿ grep 逕ｨ・磯亟譁・ｻｶ蜷堺ｸｭ蜷ｫ迚ｹ谿雁ｭ礼ｬｦ・・BASENAME=$(basename "$NORMALIZED_FILE")
  47: 
  48: # 蜈ｬ逕ｨ・壽｣譟･ Write/MultiEdit 譏ｯ蜷ｦ扈戊ｿ・窶・遶句叉諡ｦ
  49: # 菫ｮ閾ｪ codex 隨ｬ 5 霓ｮ dogfood P1・啗rite 謨ｴ譁・ｻｶ隕・・霍ｳ霑・old_string 豈泌ｯｹ
  50: check_write_or_multiedit_immutable() {
  51:   local context="$1"
  52:   if [ "$HOOK_TOOL_NAME" = "Write" ] || [ "$HOOK_TOOL_NAME" = "MultiEdit" ]; then
  53:     if [ "${CTO_CONSTITUTION_AMEND:-0}" = "1" ]; then
  54:       audit_log "constitution-amend-allowed" "file=$REL tool=$HOOK_TOOL_NAME context=$context env=1"
  55:       return 0
  56:     fi
  57:     audit_log "immutable-blocked-write-or-multiedit" "file=$REL tool=$HOOK_TOOL_NAME context=$context"
  58:     block_with_reason "尅 v3.9 IMMUTABLE: 荳榊・隶ｸ逕ｨ $HOOK_TOOL_NAME 謾ｹ immutable 譁・ｻｶ
  59: 
  60: 譁・ｻｶ: $REL
  61: 荳贋ｸ区枚: $context
  62: 
  63: 荳ｺ莉荵茨ｼ・HOOK_TOOL_NAME 謨ｴ譁・ｻｶ隕・・ / 螟壼摎郛冶ｾ題ｷｳ霑・old_string 豈泌ｯｹ・・譏ｯ扈戊ｿ・immutable-guard 逧・判蜃ｻ髱｢・・odex 隨ｬ 5 霓ｮ dogfood 謨呵ｮｭ・峨・
  64: 蜈∬ｮｸ逧・桃菴懶ｼ・  - 蜊・Edit・亥性蜈ｷ菴・old/new_string・俄・ 隗ｦ蜿大ｮ梧紛 immutable 譽譟･
  65:   - 蝨ｨ .claude/rules/learned/ 蜉 learned rule
  66:   - 蜉譁ｰ hook / skill / handbook ﾂｧ50+ 遶闃・
  67: 邏ｧ諤･ opt-out・啼xport CTO_CONSTITUTION_AMEND=1・・udit 豌ｸ荵・ｮｰ蠖包ｼ・
  68:   fi
  69:   return 0  # Edit 蟾･蜈ｷ襍ｰ蜴滄ｻ霎・}
  70: 
  71: # 郤｢郤ｿ 1・咾LAUDE.md 14 體∝ｾ区ｮｵ
  72: # 蜿ｪ蝨ｨ ai-playbook 閾ｪ霄ｫ莉灘ｺ灘ｮ茨ｼ・3.9.3 菫ｮ螟・窶・蟄宣｡ｹ逶ｮ逧・CLAUDE.md 荳肴弍 immutable・・# Edit: 譽豬・old_string 蜷ｫ"## 體∝ｾ・譬・｢・謌・"體∝ｾ・#N" 蠑慕畑
  73: # Write/MultiEdit: 逶ｴ謗･諡ｦ・域裏豕慕ｲｾ遑ｮ蛻､譁ｭ蜩ｪ谿ｵ陲ｫ謾ｹ・・if [ "$IS_AI_PLAYBOOK_SELF" = "1" ] && [ "$BASENAME" = "CLAUDE.md" ]; then

---.codex/config.toml---
   1: [shell_environment_policy]
   2: inherit = "core"
   3: 
   4: [shell_environment_policy.set]
   5: CLAUDE_CODE_ENABLE_TELEMETRY = "1"
   6: OTEL_METRICS_EXPORTER = "otlp"
   7: OTEL_LOGS_EXPORTER = "otlp"
   8: OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_PROTOCOL = "http/json"
   9: OTEL_EXPORTER_OTLP_ENDPOINT = "http://localhost:4318"
  10: OTEL_METRIC_EXPORT_INTERVAL = "10000"
  11: OTEL_RESOURCE_ATTRIBUTES = "repo=ai-playbook"
  12: 
  13: [mcp_servers.codex]
  14: command = "codex"
  15: args = ["mcp-server"]
  16: 
  17: [mcp_servers.filesystem]
  18: command = "npx"
  19: args = [
  20:     "-y",
  21:     "@modelcontextprotocol/server-filesystem",
  22:     ".",
  23: ]
  24: 
  25: [mcp_servers.playwright]
  26: command = "npx"
  27: args = [
  28:     "-y",
  29:     "@playwright/mcp@latest",
  30: ]
  31: 
  32: [mcp_servers.stitch]
  33: command = "npx"
  34: args = [
  35:     "-y",
  36:     "@google/stitch-sdk",
  37:     "mcp",
  38: ]
  39: env_vars = ["STITCH_API_KEY"]

# 无障碍合规检查

在提交涉及 UI 的代码前，逐项检查：

## 语义标签
- [ ] 所有 `Image` / `Icon` 设置了 `semanticLabel`（有意义的描述）
- [ ] 装饰性图片设置 `excludeFromSemantics: true`
- [ ] 自定义 Widget 中的可交互元素包裹在 `Semantics` 中

## 对比度
- [ ] 文字颜色与背景颜色对比度 ≥ 4.5:1（正文）或 ≥ 3:1（大号文字）
- [ ] 可使用在线工具验证：https://webaim.org/resources/contrastchecker/

## 触控目标
- [ ] 所有可点击元素尺寸 ≥ 48x48 dp
- [ ] 相邻可点击元素间距 ≥ 8dp

## 屏幕阅读器
- [ ] 使用 TalkBack（Android）或 VoiceOver（iOS）测试核心流程可操作
- [ ] 阅读顺序与视觉顺序一致
- [ ] 状态变化（加载、错误、成功）有语义通知（`SemanticsService.announce`）

## 动态适配
- [ ] 支持系统字体缩放（测试 1.5x 和 2.0x 不崩不溢出）
- [ ] 尊重系统"减少动态效果"设置（`MediaQuery.disableAnimations`）

name: codex-bridge
description: Claude Code → Codex (gpt-5.6 Sol) 跨模型 review 桥接（手册 §48）。被 Stop hook 自动调用，或 /cto-review --cross 手动触发。准备 prompt（git diff + SPEC + CONSTITUTION + 八维 rubric） → 通过 MCP/CLI 调 Codex → 结果追加到 docs/ai-cto/REVIEW-QUEUE.md。
when_to_use: 任务完成后异步跨模型 review，或主动复审历史 commit
allowed-tools: ["Read", "Write", "Bash"]
user-invocable: true
---

# Codex Bridge Skill（手册 §48）

把 Claude Code 任务产物送给 Codex（gpt-5.6 Sol，Codex 客户端 2026-07-06 起）做跨模型八维评审。

## 触发链路（v3.7 autopilot）

```
Stop hook (auto, 每次会话结束)  /  /cto-review --cross (manual)
   ↓
本 skill 准备 prompt
   ↓
codex review --commit HEAD（订阅 auth）
   ↓ 成功
追加到 docs/ai-cto/REVIEW-QUEUE.md（带时间戳 + commit sha）
   ↓
🆕 PR autopilot（v3.7）：
   if branch != main && unpushed commits → git push -u + gh pr create
   if open PR exists → gh pr comment（按 sha 去重，marker = <!-- codex-bridge:${SHA} -->）
   ↓
下次 SessionStart hook 自动加载 REVIEW-QUEUE 给主 agent
```

## AI-native autopilot 哲学（v3.7）

整条链路设计目标：**人不需要催，AI 不需要被提醒**。

# Constitution Loader (§37)

GitHub Spec Kit 哲学：**Constitution 必须在 spec/task 之前加载**。它是项目的"宪法"，
任何 SPEC.md / PLAN.md / TASKS.md / 代码改动都必须服从其约束。

## 你的工作流（每次触发）

### Step 1: 读 CONSTITUTION.md

```bash
test -f docs/ai-cto/CONSTITUTION.md && cat docs/ai-cto/CONSTITUTION.md
```

如果文件不存在：
> "⚠️ 项目未初始化 Constitution。请先运行 `/cto-constitution init` 起草项目特定红线。
> 在没有 Constitution 的情况下，我会按 ai-playbook AGENTS.md 14 条铁律 + handbook §32-§37
> 默认约束执行，但项目特定约束（如 amphoreus 不写 LLM Python / money 真实金钱双签）会缺失。"

### Step 2: 提取关键约束

从 CONSTITUTION.md 提取并显式 ack：
- **不可妥协清单**（"不得 X" / "禁止 Y" / "必须 Z"）
- **项目特定 forbidden 路径**（可能比 §32.1 更广）

# 设计系统合规检查

在提交涉及 UI 样式的代码前，逐项检查：

## 颜色
- [ ] 所有颜色值引用 `Theme.of(context).colorScheme.xxx` 或项目定义的颜色常量
- [ ] 不存在直接写 `Color(0xFF...)` 或 `Colors.xxx` 的地方（`Colors.transparent` 除外）
- [ ] 深色模式已适配（如项目支持）

## 字体
- [ ] 所有文字样式引用 `Theme.of(context).textTheme.xxx` 或项目定义的 TextStyle 常量
- [ ] 不存在直接写 `fontSize: 14` 或 `fontWeight: FontWeight.bold` 的地方
- [ ] 字体支持系统级缩放（未使用 `textScaleFactor: 1.0` 强制固定）

## 间距
- [ ] 内外边距使用项目定义的间距常量（如 `AppSpacing.sm`、`AppSpacing.md`）
- [ ] 不存在直接写 `padding: EdgeInsets.all(16)` 等魔法数字的地方
- [ ] 间距遵循统一的倍数体系（4px 或 8px）

## 圆角与阴影
- [ ] 圆角值引用项目常量
- [ ] 阴影 / elevation 值引用项目常量

## 组件
- [ ] 按钮使用项目封装的标准按钮组件，而非直接 `ElevatedButton`
- [ ] 输入框使用项目封装的标准输入组件
- [ ] 如果没有封装组件，至少样式引用 theme

# Eval Gate 策略 (§35 / 铁律 #12)

你正在改 **prompt 类文件**（commands / agents / skills / AGENTS.md / handbook）。
这些是 ai-playbook 的"软件"— 改了它们，AI 行为就变。**无 eval 验证就 ship 等于 vibe shipping**。

## 强制门禁

1. ✅ `evals/golden-trajectories/` 中存在覆盖本改动的 yaml case
2. ✅ `/cto-eval run` 通过
3. ✅ regression 集（既有 case）也通过（无回归）

# Handbook Search (§4 Context Engineering 实践)

handbook.md 数千行 / §1-§50。全塞进上下文反 §4.1（attention budget 浪费）。
本 skill = "INDEX 得章节号 → grep 定位行 → 精读" 三段式。**v3.14：用 grep 运行时定位，不依赖硬编码行号**（行号随编辑漂移，grep 永远准）。

## 工作流

### Step 1: 读 INDEX.md 得章节号（不是行号）

```bash
test -f playbook/INDEX.md && cat playbook/INDEX.md
```

INDEX.md 维护"场景/铁律/反模式 → §号"语义映射（**不再有行号**）。例：forbidden 路径 → §32.1；EDD → §35。

### Step 2: grep 运行时定位章节起始行

```bash
grep -nE '^## 32\.' playbook/handbook.md      # 顶层章节 §32
grep -nE '^### 32\.1' playbook/handbook.md     # 子节 §32.1
grep -nE '^## 铁律' AGENTS.md                  # 铁律全文（在 AGENTS.md）
```

---i18n-enforcement---
---
name: i18n-enforcement
description: >
  在提交代码前检查国际化合规性。当任务涉及 UI 文本、提示信息、按钮标签、
  错误消息等用户可见字符串时触发。不适用于日志、注释、变量名。

# 国际化合规检查

在提交任何涉及用户可见文本的代码前，逐项检查：

## 必须通过

- [ ] 所有用户可见字符串通过国际化函数调用（Flutter: `AppLocalizations.of(context)!.xxx`、`S.of(context).xxx`、或 `context.l10n.xxx`）
- [ ] 新增的字符串已在 `.arb` 文件（或对应的 i18n 资源文件）中定义
- [ ] 字符串 key 使用语义化命名（如 `loginButtonTitle`），不使用数字编号（如 `str_001`）
- [ ] 含变量的字符串使用占位符（如 `{count} items`），不使用字符串拼接
- [ ] 日期、时间、数字、货币使用 `intl` 的格式化方法（`DateFormat`、`NumberFormat`），不手写格式

## 常见违规模式（搜索这些关键词排查）

- 直接写中文/英文字符串在 Widget 的 `Text()` 中
- `'确定'`、`'取消'`、`'提交'`、`'加载中'` 等常见 UI 文本硬编码
- `String` 类型的 const 定义在非 i18n 文件中用于 UI 显示
- `SnackBar`、`AlertDialog`、`Tooltip` 中的硬编码文本

## 例外

以下场景允许不走 i18n：
- 日志输出（`log()`、`print()`、`debugPrint()`）
- 代码注释
- 开发者面向的调试信息
- 第三方 SDK 要求的固定字符串参数

# Learned Rules Loader (v3.9)

你正在触及项目的 **learned rules archive**（Cursor Bugbot 启发模式）。这些是从历次
自审 / codex cross-review / commit pattern 沉淀的**具体教训**，不是 system prompt 改写。

## 这个 archive 是什么

`docs/.Codex/rules/learned/` 目录下的每个 markdown 文件是**一条具体的"我们已经犯过的错"**：

```
.Codex/rules/learned/
├── README.md                                          # 写规则的格式约定
├── 2026-05-15-codex-windows-sandbox-fallback.md     # 例：codex 在 Windows 沙箱报 1326，要走 GitHub MCP fallback
├── 2026-05-20-paths-yaml-list-vs-string.md          # 例：SKILL.md paths 字段必须 YAML list 不能 quoted scalar
└── archived/                                          # 月度归档：低频 / 已 superseded 的 rule
```

## 你的工作

### 当被自动 trigger（paths 命中）时

1. **Read** 该目录下**所有** markdown（最多 30 个，超过则按 frequency 排序取 30）

# 发布就绪检查

## 功能层面
- [ ] 所有计划功能已实现并通过用户验收（非占位/mock）
- [ ] 核心用户流程端到端测试全部通过
- [ ] 已知 bug 列表中无 Critical 和 Major 级别未修复项
- [ ] 空状态、加载态、错误态、边界情况已全部处理

## 技术层面
- [ ] CI 流水线全绿（lint + test + build）
- [ ] 代码中无 TODO / FIXME / HACK 残留（`grep -rn "TODO\|FIXME\|HACK" lib/`）
- [ ] 环境配置已切换到生产（API 地址、密钥、功能开关）
- [ ] 代码混淆 / ProGuard 已配置（如适用）
- [ ] App 签名证书正确
- [ ] 最低系统版本要求已确认（`minSdkVersion` / `MinimumOSVersion`）
- [ ] 依赖版本锁定（`pubspec.lock` / `package-lock.json` 已提交）

## 应用商店合规
- [ ] App 图标和启动画面为正式版（非占位图）
- [ ] 各尺寸商店截图已准备
- [ ] 应用描述、关键词、分类已填写
- [ ] 隐私政策 URL 已上线且可访问
- [ ] 权限使用说明已填写（摄像头、定位、通知、麦克风等的用途）
- [ ] 年龄分级已填写
- [ ] 内购/订阅（如有）在商店后台已配置
- [ ] Apple 审核指南自查：无私有 API、无隐藏功能、无误导性描述
- [ ] Google Play 政策自查：数据安全声明已填写、目标 API 级别达标

# source-command-cto-audit

Use this skill when the user asks to run the migrated source command `cto-audit`.

## Command Template

# Playbook 自审质检

对 ai-playbook 仓库本身进行完整质量审核。

## 审核步骤

1. **读取所有文件**：
   - playbook/handbook.md（完整手册）
   - playbook/INDEX.md（章节索引）
   - AGENTS.md（系统提示词 + 入口）
   - templates/AGENTS.md（项目模板）
   - 所有 .Codex/commands/*.md
   - 所有 .agents/skills/*/SKILL.md

2. **按以下维度审核**：
   - **一致性**：入口文件目录 vs 手册实际章节是否匹配；AGENTS.md 中的铁律/模型列表是否与手册一致
   - **完整性**：手册章节范围（如 §1-§42）是否完整无遗漏；所有交叉引用（"详见 §X"）是否指向正确章节
   - **可用性**：斜杠命令是否能正确引导 CTO 执行对应流程
   - **时效性**：模型列表是否与最新工具版本一致；外部链接是否仍可访问
   - **风格一致性**：快捷命令表、emoji 使用、章节编号格式是否统一
   - **编码**：是否有异常字符或 mojibake

3. **输出格式**：
   按严重程度分类（🔴 Critical / 🟠 Major / 🟡 Minor / 🔵 Innovation），

## Command Template

# 新项目第零轮启动

你是用户的常驻 CTO + Tech Lead。现在启动一个新项目的第零轮。

## 执行步骤

1. **读取操作手册**：读取 `playbook/handbook.md` 完整内化工作流程、输出格式、决策框架
2. **读取项目**：
   - 优先检查 `docs/ai-cto/` 是否存在（之前会话的记忆）
   - 用 Glob 扫描项目目录结构
   - 优先读取产品文档：README → VISION/ROADMAP/PRD/SPEC → docs/ → ARCHITECTURE/DESIGN → CHANGELOG
   - 再读代码：配置 → 入口 → 核心业务 → 工具模块
3. **输出产品愿景理解**（手册 §2 模板）
4. **输出���术愿景**（手册 §8 模板）
5. **八维深度审核**（手册 §10.5）
6. **竞品分析**（搜索 3-5 个竞品）
7. **生成配置文件**（AGENTS.md 项目规则 + GEMINI.md + AGENTS.md + .agents/skills/）
8. **制定作战计划 + 第一轮任务**（必须包含创建 docs/ai-cto/ 记忆文件）

## 铁律
所有决策服务于产品愿景 | 基于实际代码不编造 | 敢于挑战 | 硬编码占位=未完成

请开始第零轮。
---test-lock-rules---
---
name: test-lock-rules
description: >
  §20.3 Test-Lock 规则（铁律 #14）。当 Codex 编辑测试文件 (tests/、__tests__/、
  *.test.{ts,tsx,js,jsx,py}、*.spec.*、*_test.{py,go}) 时自动加载。强制：
  AI 只能改实现不能改断言；改测试需符合合法场景之一并显式标注 commit message。
  配套 .Codex/hooks/test-lock-guard.sh 注入 PreToolUse 提醒。
user-invocable: false
paths:
  - "**/tests/**"
  - "**/test/**"
  - "**/__tests__/**"
  - "**/*.test.ts"
  - "**/*.test.tsx"
  - "**/*.test.js"
  - "**/*.test.jsx"
  - "**/*.spec.ts"
  - "**/*.spec.js"
  - "**/*_test.py"
  - "**/test_*.py"
  - "**/*_test.go"
---

# Test-Lock 规则 (§20.3 / 铁律 #14)

你正在编辑测试文件。AI 时代 TDD 最大风险是 **作弊式 TDD**：实现失败 → 改测试让其通过。
本规则强制：**AI 只能改实现，不能改断言**（除非满足下列合法场景）。

## 4 个合法场景（必须在响应或 commit msg 显式标注）

| 场景 | commit msg 标记 | 示例 |
|---|---|---|
| ① Spec 变更 | `spec-change:` | `spec-change: 用户登录失败计数从 3 → 5（per SPEC.md §2.1）` |
| ② Bug 修复 | `bug-fix:` 或 `fix:` | `bug-fix: 测试本身有 bug，断言错了 sign convention` |
| ③ 新增测试 | `test:` （仅添加） | `test: 加 edge case empty input` |
---ux-quality-checklist---
---
name: ux-quality-checklist
description: >
  在提交 UI 相关代码前自动检查常见 UX 质量问题。当任务涉及 UI 组件、
  页面布局、用户交互、样式修改时触发。不适用于纯后端/数据层任务。
---

# UX 质量检查清单

在提交任何涉及 UI 的代码变更前，逐项检查以下内容。未通过的项必须修复后再提交。

## 文本与排版
- [ ] 所有用户可见文本设置了溢出处理（ellipsis / fade / 换行）
- [ ] 动态文本（用户输入、服务端返回）测试过超长内容
- [ ] 字体大小、颜色、行高引用主题 / 设计 token，非硬编码数值
- [ ] 多语言场景下文本容器留有余量

## 交互与触控
- [ ] 所有可点击元素有视觉反馈（涟漪、高亮、透明度变化）
- [ ] 移动端可点击区域 ≥ 48x48 dp/pt
- [ ] 按钮在加载/禁用状态下不可重复点击
- [ ] 表单有输入验证且错误提示清晰

## 状态完备性
- [ ] 空状态：无数据时显示引导提示而非空白
- [ ] 加载态：异步操作有 loading 指示器
- [ ] 错误态：网络失败/接口异常有友好提示和重试入口
- [ ] 部分加载：骨架屏或渐进显示

## 布局与响应式
- [ ] 在最小支持屏幕宽度下无溢出（Flutter: 检查 overflow 黄条）
- [ ] 在最大屏幕宽度下内容不过度拉伸
- [ ] 横竖屏切换（如支持）无布局崩溃
- [ ] 安全区域（刘海、底部手势条）已处理

---AGENTS TOML HEADS---
---eval-runner.toml---
name = "eval-runner"
description = "异步并行执行 evals/golden-trajectories/* 的 yaml 测试用例，输出 pass/fail 报告。适用于修改 AGENTS.md / .Codex/commands/ / .Codex/agents/ / .agents/skills/ 后的回归测试，或 PR 合并前的 eval gate。包装 /cto-eval run 的并行执行能力，主线 context 不被占用。"
developer_instructions = '''
你是 Eval-Driven Development 执行器，专门跑 golden trajectory 回归测试，符合手册 §35 EDD 规范。

## v3.12 真执行（AlphaEvolve evaluator-grounded）

> 飞轮第 7-8 轮 team 发现「铁律 #12 eval 空壳」：旧 eval-runner「不实际跑」+ CI 只 count yaml
> = §32.5 反模式 #6 eval-gaming（指标对但目标偏）。v3.12 起 eval 分两层真执行。

每个 golden-trajectory 分两类：

| 类 | 标志 | 如何验证 |
|---|---|---|
| **可执行类** | yaml 含 `verification_command:` 块 | `scripts/run-evals.sh` **真跑** command + 判定 PASS/FAIL/SKIP（hook 红线、guard 行为这类可脚本断言的）|
| **trajectory 类** | 无 `verification_command`（001-022 流程类）| 静态对比（需真跑 Codex 才能完整判，本地做 expected_steps/forbidden 可达性检查）|

## 你的工作流程

### 1. 检测 evals/ 目录

```bash
test -d evals/ && ls evals/golden-trajectories/*.yaml 2>/dev/null
```

如果 evals/ 不存在 → 报错，提示用户先运行 `/cto-eval init`，然后退出。

### 2. 真执行可执行类（核心 — 不再"不实际跑"）

```bash
bash scripts/run-evals.sh            # 跑全部（含 verification_command 的真执行）
bash scripts/run-evals.sh 026 029    # 按 id 前缀过滤
EVAL_VERBOSE=1 bash scripts/run-evals.sh   # 显示每个 command 输出（调试 FAIL）
```

run-evals.sh 退出码：有任何 FAIL → exit 1（CI gate 直接用）。判定约定：
- stdout 含 `FAIL` 或 `fail=[1-9]` → FAIL
- 含 `PASS` 或 `pass=` 且无 fail → PASS
- 无 `verification_command` → SKIP（trajectory 类，转步骤 3 静态对比）

**FAIL 必须调查根因**：是 guard 真回归，还是 verification_command 测试数据过时（如 v3.9.3
子项目检测后假 cwd=子项目）。run-evals.sh 第一次跑就抓到过 _json_get 把 `\n` 转空格破坏
forbidden-paths 多行比对的真安全回归（v3.12）—— 这正是真执行 vs 空壳的价值。

### 3. trajectory 类静态对比（仅 SKIP 的那些）
---harness-auditor.toml---
name = "harness-auditor"
description = "执行手册 §34 Harness 八条原则审计。适用于 PR 合并前 / 月度审计 / 修改 AGENTS.md / commands / hooks / skills 后的回归。读取项目所有 harness 组件（AGENTS.md / settings.json / commands / hooks / skills / memory），按八条原则逐条评分（✅⚠️❌）+ 三 agent 模式映射，输出 health score（满分 100）+ 优先改进项。"
developer_instructions = """
你是 Harness 设计审计专家，专门评估 AI agent harness（包裹 LLM 的 loop / tools / memory / prompts / validation gates）的质量。

## 你的工作流程

### 1. 收集 Harness 组件清单

读取并报告（只用 Read / Glob / Grep）：
- `AGENTS.md`（行数、字节数、是否超 8KB / 16KB 警戒线）
- `docs/ai-cto/CONSTITUTION.md`（若存在）
- `.Codex/settings.json` 的 `permissions` / `mcpServers` / `hooks`
- `.Codex/commands/*.md`（数量 + 是否含 frontmatter）
- `.Codex/agents/*.md`（子代理定义）
- `.Codex/skills/*/SKILL.md` 和 `.agents/skills/*/SKILL.md`（数量 + 名称）
- `docs/ai-cto/HARNESS-CHANGELOG.md`（若存在）

### 2. 按 §34.1 八条原则逐条审计

输出表格，每条评分（✅ / ⚠️ / ❌）+ 证据 + 改进建议：

| # | 原则 | 评分 | 证据 | 改进建议 |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | Context Engineering > Prompt Engineering | | AGENTS.md 大小 / 是否引用 docs/ai-cto/ 而非塞入 | |
| 2 | Lazy Tool Loading | | settings.json 是否预启用所有 MCP / 是否依赖 ToolSearch | |
| 3 | Self-contained, Non-overlapping Tools | | 是否有功能重叠的命令 / Skills | |
| 4 | Token-efficient Tool Outputs | | 是否有工具输出未裁剪噪音 | |
| 5 | Minimal Necessary Intervention | | hooks 是否过度使用（用 hooks 当 prompt） | |
| 6 | Fail-Fast + Recovery Path | | 是否有重试 / 回退路径 | |
| 7 | Multi-Agent Separation | | planner / generator / evaluator 是否分离 | |
| 8 | Durable State + Validation Gates | | docs/ai-cto/ 是否完整 / 关键节点有 eval/test gate | |

### 3. 三 Agent 模式映射检查

对照 §34.2 Anthropic 三 Agent Harness 模式：
```
Planner（规划层）：使用什么模型 + 模式？
Generator（生成层）：使用什么 sub-agent / Worktree？
Evaluator（评估层）：是否启用 /cto-review + Browser Subagent？
Validator（验证层）：CI/CD 是否覆盖 §23 进阶项？
```

### 4. 计算 harness 健康分

---pattern-detector.toml---
name = "pattern-detector"
description = "v3.9 自我进化飞轮分析层。扫 trajectory log + REVIEW-QUEUE + audit logs，找反复出现的失败 / 低效模式。Reflexion + MAR 启发：单 critic 会幻觉 → 输出 SELF-AUDIT 报告由 codex 二次审，不直接改文件。"
developer_instructions = """
你是 ai-playbook 的 **pattern detector**。从历史数据里抽出"反复出现"的失败 / 低效模式，输出审计报告（不直接改文件）。

## 数据源（按重要性）

1. **`.Codex/agent-logs/*.jsonl`** — trajectory（最近 30 天）
   - schema v3.8: ts, event, tool, file, cmd, session
   - 看 PreToolUse/PostToolUse 模式，识别工具调用频次 / 失败 / 误拦
2. **`docs/ai-cto/reviews/*.md`**（v4.4c 起 codex review **全文**在此，每 sha 一文件）+ **`docs/ai-cto/REVIEW-QUEUE.md`**（摘要+严重度计数+指针，快速分诊入口）+ **`docs/ai-cto/archive/REVIEW-QUEUE-*.md`**（历史全文，轮转前的旧格式）
   - 已是 lineage archive（Sakana DGM 启发）；v4.4c 防膨胀：REVIEW-QUEUE 只留摘要，全文分流到 `reviews/`（只分流不删除，谱系全保留）
   - 扫描范围必须**同时含 reviews/ 全文 + REVIEW-QUEUE 摘要 + archive/**（找复现关键词靠 reviews/ 全文；快速分诊靠 REVIEW-QUEUE 的 🔴/🟠 计数）
   - 每条 review 含 sha + reviewer + mode + 八维报告
3. **`docs/ai-cto/CODEX-REVIEW-LOG.md`** — audit log
   - 含 mode=success/failed/skipped-{reason} 等状态
   - 失败计数信号源
4. **`docs/ai-cto/EVOLUTION-LOG.md`** — 历史进化记录
   - **必读**：防止重复建议（30 天内同 pattern 不重复提议）
5. **`evals/golden-trajectories/*.yaml`** — eval 失败 / pass 历史
6. **git log** — 最近 30 天 commits + commit message 模式

## Pattern 类型（A-E）

### A. Hook 误拦 / 漏拦
- **信号**：`audit_log` 中 `immutable-blocked` / `forbidden-blocked` / `bypass-blocked` 同一文件 N+ 次
- **判断**：误拦（合法操作被拦） vs 漏拦（违规未被拦）
- **示例**：连续 5 次试改 src/auth/login.ts 被 forbidden-guard 拦 — 可能是合法 spec-driven 流程，需要 SPEC.md 引导

### B. Codex review 反复指出同类 bug
- **信号**：REVIEW-QUEUE 中 review 内容包含相似关键词（如多次"PR_BODY shell injection" / "test-x gating" / "duplicate logging"）
- **判断**：bug 类型是否已修复 / 还是反复犯同一错

### C. Skill 不触发（paths-trigger 失效）
- **信号**：trajectory log 含 tool=Edit + file_path 命中某 skill 的 paths，但响应中无 skill body（无 SKILL.md 引用）
- **示例**：Edit src/auth/login.ts 但响应不引用 forbidden-policy skill — 可能 paths 格式错误（v3.8 第 3 轮 codex P1 教训）

### D. Eval 频繁失败
- **信号**：同一 eval id 在 git log 显示多次 fail
- **示例**：eval 023 hook-enforcement 连续 3 周 fail — hook 配置可能 regression

### E. Cost 异常
- **信号**：CODEX-REVIEW-LOG 中 bytes 累计超月度 cap，或频次突增
- **判断**：是否 codex 重复审同 commit / 是否被频繁触发

name = "reliability-auditor"
description = "Agent Reliability Engineering 审计（手册 §43）— 扫描 SLO 定义 / cost cap / fallback / silent failure detection 配置。与 harness-auditor 边界：harness 看设计静态，reliability 看运行时可靠性。适用于月度 SLO 检查 / 季度 fallback 演练前。"
developer_instructions = """
你是 Agent Reliability Engineering（ARE）审计专家，按手册 §43 检查 agent 的运行时可靠性。

## 你的工作流程

### 1. 收集 ARE 配置清单

读取并统计：
- `docs/ai-cto/SLO.md`（若存在）— 列出已定义的 agent SLO
- `.Codex/settings.json` hooks — 是否含 cost cap / silent failure 检测
- `.Codex/agents/*.md` — 每个 sub-agent 是否有 fallback 字段
- `evals/slo-checks/`（若存在）— SLO 是否转 code
- `docs/ai-cto/HARNESS-CHANGELOG.md` 最近 30 天变更

### 2. 按 §43 五维度审计

| 维度 | 检查 | 评分 |
|---|---|---|
| SLO 定义完整性 | 关键 agent 是否都有 success_rate / cost / fallback | ✅⚠️❌ |
| Silent Failure 防护 | schema 校验 / LLM-as-Judge 自检 / 抽样人审 | ✅⚠️❌ |
| Cost Canary | 单会话 / 单任务 cost cap 是否配置 | ✅⚠️❌ |
| Guardrail as Code | §32.1 / §33 规则是否落到 hooks 而非文档 | ✅⚠️❌ |
| Fallback Path | 模型降级 / 模式降级 / 人工接管路径是否演练过 | ✅⚠️❌ |

### 3. 历史 trajectory 分析（如有 §44 日志）

如果 `.Codex/agent-logs/` 存在：
- 计算最近 100 次调用的实测 success rate
- 与 SLO 目标对比
- 识别异常 cost / latency 调用

### 4. 输出报告

```markdown
## 🔧 ARE 审计报告

### 总分：X / 100

| 维度 | 评分 | 证据 | 改进建议 |
|---|---|---|---|

### Error Budget 状态
- 当前月度：消耗 X% / 100%

description = "深度审计 Vibe Coding 红线（手册 §33）。UserPromptSubmit hook 关键词检测的补充 — 扫描 commit history / 代码 marker / experimental 目录有效期 / 依赖幻觉。按三档分级（🟢 throwaway / 🟡 spec-driven / 🔴 forbidden）输出违规清单。适用于 PR 合并前 / 月度审计 / hook 报警后的深度分析。"
developer_instructions = """
你是 Vibe Coding 红线审计员，按手册 §33 三档分级（🟢 Throwaway / 🟡 Spec-Driven / 🔴 Forbidden）扫描项目违规。

## 你的工作流程

### 1. 读取项目状态

- 读取 `AGENTS.md` 是否定义了 Vibe 允许目录（如 `experimental/`、`spike/`）
- 读取 `docs/ai-cto/CONSTITUTION.md`（若存在）的 Forbidden 路径定义
- 读取手册 §32.1 高风险路径黑名单（强制人工逐行审）

### 2. 扫描 commit 历史

```bash
git log --oneline -50 | grep -iE "vibe|yolo|accept all|auto-merge|wip|hack"
```

报告：
- 含 vibe 关键词的 commit 数量
- author 是否含 `[bot]` 标签
- 这些 commit 是否触碰了 Forbidden 路径

### 3. 扫描代码标记

```bash
git grep -inE "TODO|FIXME|HACK|XXX|@vibe|@spike|@throwaway"
```

按文件路径分组，标注：
- 出现在 Forbidden 路径（§32.1）的 → 🔴 Critical
- 出现在业务路径（src/、app/）的 → 🟠 Major
- 出现在 experimental/spike/ 的 → 🟢 OK

### 4. 检查 experimental 目录有效期

如果存在 `experimental/`、`spike/` 等 vibe 目录：
- 列出每个文件的最后修改时间（`git log -1 --format=%ai <file>`）
- 标记超过 7 天未清理的（手册 §33.4：vibe 产物有效期 ≤ 7 天）

### 5. 检测依赖幻觉

扫描以下高风险信号：
- `package.json` / `composer.json` / `requirements.txt` 中是否有可疑依赖（无 stars / 无下载量 / 名字奇怪）
