 195: // ═══ forbidden-guard ═══（§32.1 forbidden 路径 → exit 2；剥离失败保留完整路径）
 196: export function forbiddenGuard(ctx) {
 197:   if (!ctx.filePath) process.exit(0);
 198:   const { normCwd, rel } = normalizePaths(ctx, 'keep');
 199:   const { pattern } = forbiddenPattern(normCwd);
 200:   if (!pattern) process.exit(0);
 201:   let re;
 202:   try { re = new RegExp(`(${pattern})`); } catch {
 203:     process.stderr.write('⚠️ forbidden pattern 编译失败，回退内置 pattern\n');
 204:     re = new RegExp(`(${FORBIDDEN_FALLBACK_PATTERN})`);
 205:   }
 206:   if (re.test(rel)) {
 207:     if (env().CTO_DOUBLE_SIGNED === '1') {
 208:       auditLog(ctx, 'forbidden-guard', 'forbidden-allowed', `path=${rel} double_signed=true`);
 209:       process.exit(0);
 210:     }
 211:     auditLog(ctx, 'forbidden-guard', 'forbidden-blocked', `path=${rel}`);
 212:     block(`🛑 §32.1 BLOCKED: \`${rel}\` 命中 forbidden 路径
 213: 
 214: 此路径禁止 vibe coding（铁律 #13），必须走 Spec-Driven：
 215:   1. /cto-spec specify — 先写 SPEC 并经人审
 216:   2. 双签：CTO + 第二模型独立审（/cto-review --cross）
 217:   3. PR 打 requires-double-review 标签
 218: 
 219: 参考：handbook §32.1 / §19 / 铁律 #13
 220: 紧急 opt-out（已获双签后）：export CTO_DOUBLE_SIGNED=1（audit 永久记录）`);
 221:   }
 222:   process.exit(0);
 223: }
 224: 
 225: // ═══ branch-guard ═══（铁律 #8：保护分支上禁 Edit；v4.0c 扩展 Bash git commit/push/merge）
 226: const PROTECTED_BRANCHES = new Set(['main', 'master', 'production', 'prod', 'release']);
 227: const PROTECTED_RE = '(main|master|production|prod|release)';
 228: 
 229: // v4.0c（需人双签的新语义）：解析真实 git 子命令，不做子串匹配（cutover 审查 MUST-2：
 230: // PR body / git log --grep / echo 文本必须放行）。剥离引号 + heredoc 后按 shell 操作符切段。
 231: function stripQuotedAndHeredoc(cmd) {
 232:   return splitLines(cmd).map((l) => l.replace(/<<-?'?[A-Za-z_]+'?.*$/, '')).join('\n')
 233:     .replace(/'[^']*'/g, ' ')
 234:     .replace(/"(\\.|[^"\\])*"/g, ' ');
 235: }
 236: 
 237: function gitSubcommand(segment) {
 238:   const toks = segment.trim().split(/\s+/);
 239:   if (toks[0] !== 'git') return { sub: '', rest: [] };
 240:   const eatsArg = new Set(['-C', '-c', '--git-dir', '--work-tree', '--namespace', '--exec-path']);
 241:   for (let i = 1; i < toks.length; i++) {
 242:     const t = toks[i];
 243:     if (t.startsWith('-')) { if (eatsArg.has(t)) i++; continue; }
 244:     return { sub: t, rest: toks.slice(i + 1) };
 245:   }
 246:   return { sub: '', rest: [] };
 247: }
 248: 
 249: // push 判定看 refspec 而非 HEAD（cutover 审查：HEAD=main 时 push feature 分支必须放行）；
 250: // bare `git push`（无 refspec）默认推当前分支 → 回退看 HEAD。
 251: function pushTargetsProtected(rest, headProtected) {
 252:   const nonFlags = rest.filter((t) => !t.startsWith('-'));
 253:   const refspecs = nonFlags.slice(1); // 第一个非 flag = remote
 254:   if (refspecs.length === 0) return headProtected;
 255:   return refspecs.some((r) => new RegExp(`(^|:)${PROTECTED_RE}$`).test(r));
 256: }
 257: 
 258: // v4.7 跨仓/复合命令感知（修实测两次 FP）：旧实现只用会话 cwd 判 HEAD，于是
 259: //   ① `cd /other/repo && git commit`（他仓已在 feature 分支）被按本仓 HEAD=main 误拦；
 260: //   ② `git checkout -b feat/x && git commit`（同串先切分支）按切换前 HEAD 误拦（TOCTOU）。
 261: // 修：按 segment 顺序跟踪「有效工作目录」（cd / git -C）与「同串内已切到非保护分支」。
 262: // fail-safe：目录解析不出 / 非 git 仓 / 任何异常 → 回退会话 cwd 的 HEAD（即旧行为，宁可拦）。
 263: function resolveDir(base, arg) {
 264:   if (!arg) return base;
 265:   const a = arg.replaceAll('\\', '/').replace(/^["']|["']$/g, '');
 266:   if (/^\/|^[A-Za-z]:\//.test(a)) return a.replace(/\/+$/, '') || a; // 绝对路径
 267:   const b = String(base || '.').replaceAll('\\', '/').replace(/\/+$/, '');
 268:   const parts = b.split('/');
 269:   for (const seg of a.split('/')) {
 270:     if (!seg || seg === '.') continue;
 271:     if (seg === '..') { if (parts.length > 1) parts.pop(); continue; }
 272:     parts.push(seg);
 273:   }
 274:   return parts.join('/') || b;
 275: }
 276: 
 277: function branchGuardBash(ctx) {
 278:   if (!ctx.cmd) process.exit(0);
 279:   const sessionBranch = gitBranch(ctx.cwd);
 280:   const segments = stripQuotedAndHeredoc(ctx.cmd).split(/&&|\|\||;|\||\n/);
 281:   let curDir = ctx.cwd;          // 有效工作目录（随 cd 推进）
 282:   let switchedToSafe = false;    // 同串内已 checkout/switch 到非保护分支
 283:   const branchCache = new Map();
 284:   const branchOf = (dir) => {
 285:     const k = String(dir);
 286:     if (!branchCache.has(k)) {
 287:       let b = '';
 288:       try {
 289:         // 目录必须真实存在才按它判 —— 否则 gitBranch 内部会回退到「进程 cwd」，
 290:         // 那是 guard 自己的运行目录，与被检命令无关 → 会漏拦。fail-safe 用会话 HEAD。
 291:         if (fs.statSync(fsPath(String(dir).replaceAll('\\', '/'))).isDirectory()) b = gitBranch(dir);
 292:       } catch { b = ''; }
 293:       branchCache.set(k, b || sessionBranch);
 294:     }
 295:     return branchCache.get(k);
 296:   };
 297: 
 298:   for (const seg of segments) {
 299:     const toks = seg.trim().split(/\s+/);
 300:     // cd 跟踪（cd - / 无参 → 不改，保守）
 301:     if (toks[0] === 'cd' && toks[1] && toks[1] !== '-') { curDir = resolveDir(curDir, toks[1]); continue; }
 302:     const { sub, rest } = gitSubcommand(seg);
 303:     // 同串内切分支：-b/-c 新建 或 切到非保护分支 → 后续 commit 视为安全；切回保护分支 → 复位
 304:     if (sub === 'checkout' || sub === 'switch') {
 305:       const names = rest.filter((t) => !t.startsWith('-'));
 306:       const target = rest.includes('-b') || rest.includes('-c') || rest.includes('-B') ? names[0] : names[0];
 307:       if (target) switchedToSafe = !PROTECTED_BRANCHES.has(target);
 308:       continue;
 309:     }
 310:     // -C <dir> 覆盖本 segment 的目录
 311:     const ci = toks.indexOf('-C');
 312:     const segDir = ci > 0 && toks[ci + 1] ? resolveDir(curDir, toks[ci + 1]) : curDir;
 313:     const branch = switchedToSafe ? '' : branchOf(segDir);
 314:     const headProtected = PROTECTED_BRANCHES.has(branch);
 315:     let hit = '';
 316:     if ((sub === 'commit' || sub === 'merge') && headProtected) hit = `git ${sub}（HEAD=${branch}）`;
 317:     if (sub === 'push' && pushTargetsProtected(rest, headProtected)) hit = `git push → 保护分支 refspec（HEAD=${branch}）`;
 318:     if (hit) {
 319:       if (env().CTO_MAIN_EDIT_ALLOWED === '1') {
 320:         auditLog(ctx, 'branch-guard', 'main-commit-allowed-emergency', `branch=${branch} cmd=${headBytes(ctx.cmd, 200)}`);
 321:         process.exit(0);
 322:       }
 323:       auditLog(ctx, 'branch-guard', 'main-commit-blocked', `branch=${branch} hit=${hit} cmd=${headBytes(ctx.cmd, 200)}`);
 324:       deny(`🛑 铁律 #8 BLOCKED: ${hit}
 325: 
 326: 命令：\`${headBytes(ctx.cmd, 300)}\`
 327: 
 328: 保护分支（main/master/production/prod/release）不接受直接 commit/merge/push —
 329: 先建分支走 PR：git checkout -b feat/<short-name>
 330: 
 331: 紧急 opt-out：export CTO_MAIN_EDIT_ALLOWED=1（audit 永久记录）
 332: 参考：CLAUDE.md 铁律 #8 / v4.0c 双签语义`);
 333:     }
 334:   }
 335:   process.exit(0);
 336: }
 337: 
 338: // v4.0e（修 2026-07-02 误拦 + codex §48 加固×2）：判定目标文件是否落在当前 git 工作树内。
 339: // 保护分支只保护本仓工作树 — 仓库外文件（如 ~/.claude/.../memory/*.md）与本仓 main 无关 → 放行。
 340: // 工作树根 = 从 cwd 按 `git rev-parse --show-cdup` 相对上爬（非 --show-toplevel 的 resolved-real 路径）：
---340-470---
 340: // 工作树根 = 从 cwd 按 `git rev-parse --show-cdup` 相对上爬（非 --show-toplevel 的 resolved-real 路径）：
 341: //   - Major-1（cwd 为子目录）：cdup 上爬到真根 → cwd 外的同仓文件仍拦；
 342: //   - round-3（symlink/junction 别名）：全程停留在 cwd/file 的路径空间，不引入 real 路径 → 别名前缀仍匹配。
 343: // canonPath 归一：MSYS(/c/)→原生盘符 + 去尾斜杠 + Windows 大小写不敏感（Major-2）。
 344: // engine 与 legacy branch-guard.sh 的 _canon / cdup 上爬同款，保 parity。
 345: function canonPath(p) {
 346:   let s = fsPath(String(p).replaceAll('\\', '/')).replace(/\/+$/, '');
 347:   if (process.platform === 'win32') s = s.toLowerCase(); // Windows FS 大小写不敏感
 348:   return s;
 349: }
 350: function fileInsideWorktree(ctx) {
 351:   const rawFile = String(ctx.filePath).replaceAll('\\', '/');
 352:   if (!/^\/|^[A-Za-z]:\//.test(rawFile)) return true; // 相对路径 → 相对 cwd（恒在工作树内）
 353:   // 从 cwd 上爬 N 层得工作树根（N = cdup 中 '..' 段数）；全程 cwd 空间，与 file_path 同空间
 354:   let root = String(ctx.cwd || '.').replaceAll('\\', '/').replace(/\/+$/, '');
 355:   const cdup = gitCdup(ctx.cwd);
 356:   if (cdup) {
 357:     const levels = cdup.split('/').filter((s) => s === '..').length;
 358:     for (let i = 0; i < levels; i++) root = root.replace(/\/[^/]+$/, '');
 359:   }
 360:   const base = canonPath(root);
 361:   const f = canonPath(rawFile);
 362:   return f === base || f.startsWith(base + '/');
 363: }
 364: 
 365: export function branchGuard(ctx) {
 366:   if (ctx.toolName === 'Bash') return branchGuardBash(ctx);
 367:   if (!ctx.filePath) process.exit(0);
 368:   const branch = gitBranch(ctx.cwd);
 369:   if (!branch) process.exit(0); // 非 git repo / detached 空值 → 放行（与 bash 一致）
 370:   if (PROTECTED_BRANCHES.has(branch)) {
 371:     // v4.0e：仓库外文件放行（保护分支只拦本仓工作树内的直接 Edit/Write）
 372:     if (!fileInsideWorktree(ctx)) {
 373:       auditLog(ctx, 'branch-guard', 'main-edit-outside-repo-allowed', `branch=${branch} file=${ctx.filePath}`);
 374:       process.exit(0);
 375:     }
 376:     if (env().CTO_MAIN_EDIT_ALLOWED === '1') {
 377:       auditLog(ctx, 'branch-guard', 'main-edit-allowed-emergency', `branch=${branch} file=${ctx.filePath}`);
 378:       process.exit(0);
 379:     }
 380:     auditLog(ctx, 'branch-guard', 'main-edit-blocked', `branch=${branch} file=${ctx.filePath}`);
 381:     block(`🛑 铁律 #8 BLOCKED: 当前在 \`${branch}\` 分支上直接 Edit
 382: 
 383: 文件：${ctx.filePath}
 384: 
 385: 先创建分支再动手：
 386:   git checkout -b feat/<short-name>
 387: 
 388: 紧急 opt-out：export CTO_MAIN_EDIT_ALLOWED=1（audit 永久记录）
 389: 参考：CLAUDE.md 铁律 #8`);
 390:   }
 391:   process.exit(0);
 392: }

 394: // ═══ test-lock-guard ═══（铁律 #14：advisory only，永不 block）
 395: const TEST_PATTERN = /\/tests?\/|\/__tests__\/|\.test\.[jt]sx?$|\.spec\.[jt]sx?$|_test\.py$|test_[^/]+\.py$|_test\.go$|.*Test\.java$|.*Spec\.scala$/;
 396: export function testLockGuard(ctx) {
 397:   if (!ctx.filePath) process.exit(0);
 398:   const { rel } = normalizePaths(ctx, 'basename');
 399:   if (TEST_PATTERN.test(rel)) {
 400:     if (env().CTO_TEST_LOCK_ACK === '1') {
 401:       auditLog(ctx, 'test-lock-guard', 'test-lock-ack', `file=${rel}`);
 402:       process.exit(0);
 403:     }
 404:     auditLog(ctx, 'test-lock-guard', 'test-lock-warn', `file=${rel}`);
 405:     remind(`🛑 §20.3 Test-Lock 触发（铁律 #14）: ${rel}
 406: 
 407: 测试文件锁定：AI 只能改实现，不能改断言迁就实现（作弊式 TDD）。
 408: 合法修改场景（需在 commit message 声明依据）：
 409:   - spec-change: SPEC.md 验收标准变了
 410:   - bug-fix: 测试本身有 bug（断言错 / edge case 漏）
 411:   - 新增测试 / refactor（不改断言语义）
 412: 
 413: 非法：实现失败 → 改测试让它通过 / 删"麻烦的"测试。
 414: 确认合法：export CTO_TEST_LOCK_ACK=1（audit 永久记录）
 415: 参考：handbook §20.3 / 铁律 #14 / .claude/rules/test-lock.md`, 'PreToolUse');
 416:   }
 417:   process.exit(0);
 418: }

 420: // ═══ eval-gate ═══（铁律 #12：PostToolUse advisory）
 421: const PROMPT_PATTERN = /\.claude\/commands\/|\.claude\/agents\/|\.claude\/skills\/|\.agents\/skills\/|^CLAUDE\.md$|\/CLAUDE\.md$|playbook\/handbook\.md$|\.claude\/output-styles\//;
 422: export function evalGate(ctx) {
 423:   if (!ctx.filePath) process.exit(0);
 424:   const { rel } = normalizePaths(ctx, 'basename');
 425:   if (PROMPT_PATTERN.test(rel)) {
 426:     auditLog(ctx, 'eval-gate', 'eval-gate-warn', `file=${rel}`); // audit 先于 opt-out（顺序 load-bearing）
 427:     if (env().CTO_EVAL_GATE_ACK === '1') process.exit(0);
 428:     remind(`📊 §35 / 铁律 #12 触发: ${rel}
 429: 
 430: 你刚才改了 agent 配置类文件。无 eval 不进 main（铁律 #12）：
 431:   1. evals/golden-trajectories/ 需有覆盖本改动的 case
 432:   2. 合并前跑 \`/cto-eval run\`（或 bash scripts/run-evals.sh）
 433:   3. regression 集也要过（无回归）
 434: 
 435: 已有 eval 覆盖 / 纯 typo：export CTO_EVAL_GATE_ACK=1
 436: 参考：handbook §35 / 铁律 #12 / .claude/rules/eval-gate.md`, 'PostToolUse');
 437:   }
 438:   process.exit(0);
 439: }

 441: // ═══ vibe-prompt-guard ═══（§33 关键词提醒；无 override / 无 opt-out）
 442: // 注意：\b 对 CJK 在 JS 不成立（\w 仅 ASCII）→ 强行 单独用无边界匹配（与 GNU grep locale 行为的最接近可用语义）
 443: const VIBE_ASCII = /\byolo\b|\baccept all\b|\bvibe ship\b|--no-verify|\bskip tests\b|\bjust do it\b|\bno spec\b/i;
 444: export function vibePromptGuard(ctx) {
 445:   if (!ctx.prompt) process.exit(0);
 446:   if (VIBE_ASCII.test(ctx.prompt) || ctx.prompt.includes('强行')) {
 447:     auditLog(ctx, 'vibe-prompt-guard', 'vibe-keyword', headBytes(ctx.prompt, 100));
 448:     remind(`⚠️ §33 红线提醒：检测到 vibe 关键词
 449: 
 450: 三档分级（handbook §33）：
 451:   🟢 throwaway 原型 → 可以 vibe，但产物进 experimental/ 且 7 天过期
 452:   🟡 生产代码 → 必须 spec-driven（/cto-spec specify）
 453:   🔴 Forbidden 路径（auth/支付/secrets/migration/crypto/infra）禁止 vibe coding（铁律 #13）
 454: 
 455: .claude/hooks/forbidden-guard.sh 会硬阻止 Edit forbidden 路径。
 456: 参考：handbook §33 / 铁律 #13`, 'UserPromptSubmit');
 457:   }
 458:   process.exit(0);

 461: // ═══ trajectory-logger ═══（PostToolUse *：脱敏 jsonl；schema v3.8 字节兼容）
 462: export function trajectoryLogger(ctx) {
 463:   const cwd = fsPath((ctx.cwd || '.').replaceAll('\\', '/'));
 464:   const dir = `${cwd}/.claude/agent-logs`;
 465:   try { if (!fs.statSync(dir).isDirectory()) process.exit(0); } catch { process.exit(0); }
 466:   const rawCmd = env().CTO_TRAJECTORY_FULL === '1' ? ctx.cmd : headBytes(ctx.cmd, 200);
 467:   const line = `{"ts":"${isoLocal()}","schema":"v3.8","event":"${escapeField(ctx.event)}","tool":"${escapeField(ctx.toolName)}","file":"${escapeField(ctx.filePath)}","cmd":"${escapeField(rawCmd)}","session":"${escapeField(ctx.sessionId)}"}\n`;
 468:   try { fs.appendFileSync(`${dir}/${localDay()}.jsonl`, line); } catch { /* 静默 */ }
 469:   process.exit(0);
 470: }
---470-620---
 470: }
 471: 
 472: // ═══ bypass-guard ═══（PreToolUse Bash：hook 绕过尝试 → deny JSON）
 473: // 单源：pattern 字符串由 lib.mjs BYPASS_PATTERNS 提供（= common.sh bypass_patterns()）。
 474: // /m 保留 legacy grep 逐行语义（($|\s) 的 $ 需匹配行尾而非串尾）。
 475: const BYPASS_PATTERNS = new RegExp(BYPASS_PATTERNS_SRC, 'm');
 476: export function bypassGuard(ctx) {
 477:   if (ctx.toolName !== 'Bash') process.exit(0);
 478:   if (!ctx.cmd) process.exit(0);
 479:   // v4.4b 引号插入逃逸硬化：shell 执行前吃掉引号/反斜杠字符，guard 必须看到 shell 看到的形态 ——
 480:   // `core.hooks'Path'` / `"core.hooksPath"` / `core\.hooksPath` / 引号包 metachar 值 都归一为可命中串。
 481:   // 广义 core.hooksPath token + 本剥字符 = 对全部 3 轮对抗验证的引号/续行逃逸免疫（子串仍在即命中）。
 482:   // 只删不增 → 匹配面严格超集，原命中不丢失。与 legacy bypass-guard.sh tr -d 逐字节同步。DECISIONS ADR-010。
 483:   const scanCmd = ctx.cmd.replace(/['"\\]/g, '');
 484:   if (BYPASS_PATTERNS.test(scanCmd)) {
 485:     if (env().CTO_BYPASS_ALLOWED === '1') {
 486:       auditLog(ctx, 'bypass-guard', 'bypass-allowed-emergency', `cmd=${ctx.cmd}`);
 487:       process.exit(0);
 488:     }
 489:     auditLog(ctx, 'bypass-guard', 'bypass-blocked', `cmd=${ctx.cmd}`);
 490:     deny(`🛑 BLOCKED: 检测到 hook/pre-commit 绕过尝试
 491: 
 492: 命令：\`${ctx.cmd}\`
 493: 
 494: pre-commit hook 不可绕过（Constitution 安全宪法 #3 / GitHub #40117 已知绕过面）。
 495: hook 失败 → 修根因，不是跳过检查。
 496: 
 497: 紧急 opt-out（人已确认）：export CTO_BYPASS_ALLOWED=1（audit 永久记录）
 498: 参考：https://github.com/anthropics/claude-code/issues/40117`);
 499:   }
 500:   process.exit(0);
 501: }
 502: 
 503: // ═══ destructive-action-guard ═══（PreToolUse Bash：不可逆动作 → deny JSON）
 504: // SCAN_CMD：仅剥离 heredoc 起始标记至行尾（v3.11 定案：引号内容保留 — psql -c "DROP..." 必须命中）
 505: const FS_PATTERNS = `rm\\s+-rf\\s+["']?/($|\\s|["'])|rm\\s+-rf\\s+["']?~($|\\s|["'])|rm\\s+-rf\\s+["']?\\$HOME|rm\\s+-rf\\s+["']?\\.\\s|rm\\s+-rf\\s+["']?\\*($|\\s)|find\\s+/?\\s.*-delete|>\\s*/dev/sda|mkfs|dd\\s+if=.*of=/dev/`;
 506: const DB_PATTERNS = `${DESTRUCTIVE_SQL_CORE}|psql.*-c.*DROP|mongo.*dropDatabase|redis-cli.*FLUSHALL`;
 507: const CLOUD_PATTERNS = `terraform\\s+destroy|vercel\\s+rm\\s.*--yes|railway\\s+(down|destroy)|supabase\\s+project\\s+delete|aws\\s+s3\\s+rb\\s+["']?s3://.*--force|aws\\s+rds\\s+delete-db-instance|aws\\s+ec2\\s+terminate-instances.*--force|gh\\s+repo\\s+delete|gh\\s+secret\\s+remove|firebase\\s+(use\\s+.*&&.*deploy|projects:delete)|heroku\\s+apps:destroy|fly\\s+apps\\s+destroy|kubectl\\s+delete\\s+(ns|namespace|cluster|all)|docker\\s+system\\s+prune\\s+--all\\s+--volumes`;
 508: const COMBINED_DESTRUCTIVE = new RegExp(`${FS_PATTERNS}|${DB_PATTERNS}|${CLOUD_PATTERNS}`, 'im');
 509: 
 510: export function destructiveActionGuard(ctx) {
 511:   if (ctx.toolName !== 'Bash') process.exit(0);
 512:   if (!ctx.cmd) process.exit(0);
 513:   const scanCmd = splitLines(ctx.cmd).map((l) => l.replace(/<<-?'?[A-Za-z_]+'?.*$/, '')).join('\n');
 514:   // 纯 echo/printf 输出（无 shell 操作符）→ 放行（v3.10.2 教训：文本不是执行）
 515:   if (/^[ \t]*(echo|printf)[ \t]/m.test(scanCmd) && !/&&|\|\||;|\$\(|\|\s/.test(scanCmd)) {
 516:     process.exit(0);
 517:   }
 518:   if (COMBINED_DESTRUCTIVE.test(scanCmd)) {
 519:     if (env().CTO_DESTRUCTIVE_CONFIRMED === '1') {
 520:       auditLog(ctx, 'destructive-action-guard', 'destructive-action-allowed', `cmd=${headBytes(ctx.cmd, 200)} env=1`);
 521:       process.exit(0);
 522:     }
 523:     auditLog(ctx, 'destructive-action-guard', 'destructive-action-blocked', `cmd=${headBytes(ctx.cmd, 200)}`);
 524:     deny(`🛑 v3.10.1 DESTRUCTIVE ACTION BLOCKED
 525: 
 526: 命令：\`${headBytes(ctx.cmd, 300)}\`
 527: 
 528: 命中不可逆动作模式（FS 抹除 / DB DROP / 云资源销毁）。
 529: 参考：OWASP ASI01 / PocketOS 事故（Cursor+Claude Opus 4.6 删除生产库，theregister 2026-01）。
 530: 
 531: 确认是有意为之：export CTO_DESTRUCTIVE_CONFIRMED=1（单次，audit 永久记录）`);
 532:   }
 533:   process.exit(0);
 534: }
 535: 
 536: // ═══ mcp-guard ═══（PreToolUse mcp__.*：工具名语义 + SQL 内容 + filesystem 红线重跑）
 537: const DESTRUCTIVE_MCP_TOOL = /_(delete|drop|destroy|purge|wipe)($|_)|_delete_|delete_(branch|project|database|namespace|bucket|file|table|deployment|secret)|apply_migration|reset_branch/i;
 538: const MCP_FS_WRITE = /__(write_file|edit_file|move_file|create_file|create_directory)$/i;
 539: const DESTRUCTIVE_SQL = new RegExp(`${DESTRUCTIVE_SQL_CORE}|\\bUPDATE\\s+[a-z_]+\\s+SET\\b.*(;|$)`, 'im');
 540: const SQL_WHERE_CARVEOUT = /DELETE\s+FROM.*\bWHERE\b|UPDATE\s+.*\bWHERE\b/i;
 541: const SQL_HARD = /\bDROP\b|\bTRUNCATE\b/i;
 542: const MCP_TEST_PATTERN = /\/tests?\/|\/__tests__\/|\.test\.[jt]sx?$|\.spec\.[jt]sx?$|_test\.py$|test_[^/]+\.py$|_test\.go$/;
 543: 
 544: export function mcpGuard(ctx) {
 545:   if (!ctx.toolName.startsWith('mcp__')) process.exit(0);
 546:   let blocked = false;
 547:   let reason = '';
 548: 
 549:   // Check 1：工具名 destructive 语义
 550:   if (DESTRUCTIVE_MCP_TOOL.test(ctx.toolName)) {
 551:     blocked = true;
 552:     reason = `MCP 工具名命中 destructive 语义: ${ctx.toolName}`;
 553:   }
 554: 
 555:   // Check 2：execute_sql 类 query/sql 参数内容（A4 决议：不扫 description 元数据 —
 556:   // PreToolUse stdin 不含 MCP 工具 description，扫描是 no-op = 虚假安全感，见 learned rule 2026-05-30）
 557:   const sqlText = `${ctx.query}${ctx.query && ctx.sql ? ' ' : ''}${ctx.sql}`;
 558:   if (!blocked && sqlText && DESTRUCTIVE_SQL.test(sqlText)) {
 559:     if (SQL_WHERE_CARVEOUT.test(sqlText) && !SQL_HARD.test(sqlText)) {
 560:       // 带 WHERE 的 DELETE/UPDATE 且无 DROP/TRUNCATE → carve-out 放行
 561:     } else {
 562:       blocked = true;
 563:       reason = `MCP SQL 含 destructive 操作: ${headBytes(sqlText, 150)}`;
 564:     }
 565:   }
 566: 
 567:   // Check 3：MCP filesystem 写类工具 → 重跑三条文件红线（learned rule 2026-05-29：path 字段）
 568:   if (MCP_FS_WRITE.test(ctx.toolName) && ctx.filePath) {
 569:     const { normFile, normCwd, rel } = normalizePaths(ctx, 'basename');
 570:     if (!blocked && new RegExp('docs/ai-cto/CONSTITUTION\\.md|scripts/forbidden-paths\\.txt').test(`${rel} ${normFile}`)) {
 571:       blocked = true;
 572:       reason = `MCP filesystem 写 immutable 文件: ${rel}（绕过 immutable-guard）`;
 573:     }
 574:     if (!blocked) {
 575:       const { pattern } = forbiddenPattern(normCwd);
 576:       let re = null;
 577:       try { re = pattern ? new RegExp(`(${pattern})`) : null; } catch { re = new RegExp(`(${FORBIDDEN_FALLBACK_PATTERN})`); }
 578:       if (re && re.test(rel) && env().CTO_DOUBLE_SIGNED !== '1') {
 579:         blocked = true;
 580:         reason = `MCP filesystem 写 forbidden 路径: ${rel}（绕过 forbidden-guard）`;
 581:       }
 582:     }
 583:     if (!blocked && env().CTO_TEST_LOCK_ACK !== '1' && MCP_TEST_PATTERN.test(rel)) {
 584:       blocked = true;
 585:       reason = `MCP filesystem 写测试文件: ${rel}（绕过 test-lock-guard，§20.3）`;
 586:     }
 587:   }
 588: 
 589:   if (blocked) {
 590:     if (env().CTO_MCP_DESTRUCTIVE_CONFIRMED === '1') {
 591:       auditLog(ctx, 'mcp-guard', 'mcp-destructive-allowed', `tool=${ctx.toolName} env=1`);
 592:       process.exit(0);
 593:     }
 594:     auditLog(ctx, 'mcp-guard', 'mcp-destructive-blocked', `tool=${ctx.toolName} reason=${reason}`);
 595:     deny(`🛑 v3.11 MCP DESTRUCTIVE BLOCKED
 596: 
 597: ${reason}
 598: 
 599: MCP 工具权限往往比 Bash 更大（直连生产 DB / 云资源），enforcement 必须覆盖（OWASP ASI04）。
 600: 只读操作（list_/get_/search_/SELECT）不受影响。
 601: 
 602: 确认是有意为之：export CTO_MCP_DESTRUCTIVE_CONFIRMED=1（单次，audit 永久记录）`);
 603:   }
 604:   process.exit(0);
 605: }

 340: test('branch-Bash: v4.7 跨仓 cd / git -C —— 按目标仓 HEAD 判定（修实测 FP）', () => {
 341:   const mainDir = mainRepo();      // 会话 cwd（在 main）
 342:   const featDir = mainRepo();      // 另一仓
 343:   spawnSync('git', ['checkout', '-b', 'feat/x'], { cwd: featDir });

  20: 5. **Eval 即 fitness**：所有 agent 配置改动须配 golden trajectory（§35 + 铁律 #12）
  21: 
  22: ## 安全宪法
  23: 
  24: 1. **Forbidden 路径必须 spec-driven**（§32.1 + 铁律 #13）：auth / payment / secrets / migration / crypto / infra / .github/workflows
  25: 2. **Self-modify system prompt 禁止**（OWASP Agentic Top 10 / AIVSS）：AI 不得改 CLAUDE.md 14 铁律段 / CONSTITUTION.md / forbidden-paths.txt 删条目 / handbook §32-§35
  26: 3. **Pre-commit hook 不可绕过**（铁律 #14 + bypass-guard）：禁止 --no-verify / core.hooksPath / HUSKY=0 / stash 绕过
  27: 4. **Test-Lock 不可绕过**（铁律 #14）：AI 仅可改实现不可改测试断言
  28: 5. **Cost cap**：月度 codex token < $20，超 cap 退化为只 detect 不 codex
  29: 
  30: ## 合规宪法
  31: 
  32: 1. **Spec-Kit 对齐**（§37.3）：specify → plan → tasks，每阶段都先读 Constitution
  33: 2. **AAIF AGENTS.md**（Linux Foundation 2025-12）：跟进规范（v3.10+）
  34: 3. **审计可追溯**：所有 immutable-guard / forbidden-guard / bypass-guard 拦截写 `.claude/agent-logs/*.jsonl`
  35: 4. **GitHub Branch Protection**：main 分支必须 PR + codex review + 人 merge
  36: 
  37: ## 质量宪法
  38: 
  39: 1. **Health Score ≥ 90**（v3.9 现状 94/100）
  40: 2. **Eval pass rate ≥ 90%**（可执行类全 pass；数量见 `docs/ai-cto/COUNTS.md`）
  41: 3. **Test coverage**：核心 hooks 必须有端到端 eval
  42: 4. **Codex dogfood**：每个 PR 必跑 §48 跨模型审
  43: 5. **Failure budget**：连续 3 周相同 pattern 未采纳 → P0 升级人审
  44: 
  45: ## 不可妥协清单（immutable-guard 守护）
  46: 
  47: | 文件 | 守护级别 | 修改条件 |
  48: |---|---|---|
  49: | CLAUDE.md 14 铁律段 | 🔴 不可修改 | `CTO_CONSTITUTION_AMEND=1` + 人决策 |
  50: | docs/ai-cto/CONSTITUTION.md | 🔴 不可修改 | `/cto-constitution review` + 双签 |
  51: | scripts/forbidden-paths.txt | 🟠 仅可加，不可删 | `CTO_FORBIDDEN_REMOVE=1` 紧急解锁 |
  52: | playbook/handbook.md §32-§35 | 🔴 不可修改 | 加新章节 §50+ 反而推荐 |
  53: | .claude/hooks/*.sh 的 `block_with_reason` 调用 | 🔴 不可绕过 | 禁止移除红线逻辑 |
  54: | main / master / production / prod / release 分支 | 🔴 禁止直 Edit | `CTO_MAIN_EDIT_ALLOWED=1` 紧急解锁 |
  55: 
  56: ## Spec-Kit 映射（§37.3）
  57: 
  58: | Spec-Kit 阶段 | ai-playbook 对应 | Constitution 检查 |
  59: |---|---|---|
  60: | `/specify` | `/cto-spec specify` | spec 起草前先加载 Constitution |
  61: | `/plan` | `/cto-spec plan` | plan 步骤必须服从 Constitution |
  62: | `/tasks` | `/cto-spec tasks` | task 不能违反任何"不得 X" |
  63: | `/implement` | 直接 Edit/Write | 实施前最后一次 Constitution 校验 |
  64:

  58: ## 记忆系统
  59: 
  60: 本仓（ai-playbook 自身 SELF 记忆）实际持久化在 `docs/ai-cto/` 的文件：
  61: - CONSTITUTION.md — 项目宪法（不可妥协约束）
  62: - STATUS.md — 进度、质量评分、待办（最频繁更新）
  63: - COUNTS.md — 组件计数 SSOT
  64: - EVOLUTION-LOG.md — append-only 进化记录
  65: - HARNESS-CHANGELOG.md — harness 变更日志
  66: - SLO.md — 可靠性目标 + 季度演练
  67: - DECISIONS.md — ADR 风格决策记录
  68: - REVIEW-QUEUE.md — 跨模型 review 队列（历史按季度轮转到 `archive/`）
  69: 
  70: > TARGET 项目（被 `/cto-init` 初始化的下游仓）可经 `/cto-start` 逐步长出更完整的记忆集
  71: > （PRODUCT-VISION / TECH-VISION / ARCHITECTURE / COMPETITOR-ANALYSIS / REVIEW-BACKLOG / TECH-STACK）——
  72: > 那是面向目标项目的 aspirational 契约，不代表本仓已有这些文件。
  73: 
  74: 新会话恢复时优先读取 docs/ai-cto/，然后验证是否过时。
  75: 
  76: ## 配置生态
  77: 
  78: - **Codex**: AGENTS.md + .Codex/settings.json + .Codex/commands/ + .Codex/agents/ + .Codex/rules/ + .Codex/skills/
  79: - **Antigravity**: GEMINI.md + .agents/rules/*.md + .agents/skills/
  80: - **Codex**: AGENTS.md + .agents/skills/ + config.toml
  81: - **共用 Skills**: `.agents/skills/`（跨平台）+ `.Codex/skills/`（Codex 原生，通过 scripts/sync-skills.sh 同步 — `.Codex/skills` 为 SSOT，`--check` 校验）
  82: 
  83: ## 路径触发规则（按需加载）
  84: 
  85: - `.Codex/rules/forbidden-paths.md` — 触及 auth/payment/secrets/migration/crypto 时强制双签（§32.1）
  86: - `.Codex/rules/test-lock.md` — 编辑测试文件时检查 5 条防作弊规则（§20.3 / 铁律 #14）
  87: - `.Codex/rules/eval-gate.md` — 修改 commands/agents/skills/AGENTS.md 时提醒 eval 门禁（§35 / 铁律 #12）
  88: 
  89: ## 自动化 vs 手动命令
  90: 
  91: > 大部分检查由 `.Codex/settings.json` 中的 hooks **自动触发**（§41）。下面这些命令是**决策入口或深度审计**，仅在需要时手动调用（完整计数见 `docs/ai-cto/COUNTS.md`）。
  92: 
  93: **Hooks 自动接管的场景**（无需手动）：
  94: - 会话启动 → 自动加载 `docs/ai-cto/CONSTITUTION.md` + `STATUS.md`
  95: - 用户输入含 vibe 关键词 → 自动提示 §33 红线
  96: - 编辑 `tests/**` → 自动提示 §20.3 Test-Lock
  97: - 编辑 forbidden 路径（auth/支付/secrets/migration）→ 自动提示双签
  98: - 编辑 AGENTS.md / commands / skills → 自动提示需跑 eval
  99: - git commit 触及高风险路径 → 自动提醒 vibe-check
 100: - 会话结束 → 自动输出未提交改动摘要

name: Eval Gate (铁律 #12)

# 修改 commands / agents / skills / CLAUDE.md / handbook / 计数 SSOT 时跑全量 eval
on:
  pull_request:
    paths:
      - '.claude/commands/**'
      - '.claude/agents/**'
      - '.claude/skills/**'
      - '.agents/skills/**'
      - 'CLAUDE.md'
      - 'playbook/handbook.md'
      - 'evals/**'
      - 'scripts/run-evals.sh'
      - 'scripts/check-counts.sh'
      - 'docs/ai-cto/COUNTS.md'
      - '.claude/hooks/**'
  # v4.1 (SPEC-001 item 3): 闭合 push-不走-PR 缺口 —— 直接 push 到 main 也跑 eval gate。
  # 事后信号（push 已发生）；真正阻断靠 GitHub branch protection（require PR + require this
  # check），属仓库治理开关（改变人的 direct-push 权限）—— 见 SPEC-001 tasks 人工步。
  push:
    branches: [main]
    paths:
      - '.claude/commands/**'
      - '.claude/agents/**'
      - '.claude/skills/**'
      - '.agents/skills/**'
      - 'CLAUDE.md'
      - 'playbook/handbook.md'
      - 'evals/**'
      - 'scripts/run-evals.sh'
      - 'scripts/check-counts.sh'
      - 'docs/ai-cto/COUNTS.md'
      - '.claude/hooks/**'
  workflow_dispatch:

jobs:
  eval-gate:
    name: Run evals (v3.12 真执行)
    runs-on: ubuntu-latest
    steps:
      - uses: actions/checkout@v4

      # v4.0e: 显式声明 Node（v4.0b guard engine = engine/*.mjs，run-evals.sh 经 shim exec node）。
      # ubuntu-latest 预装 node，但显式 setup 避免 base image 变更时静默破门（cutover 审查 MUST）。
      - uses: actions/setup-node@v4
        with:
          node-version: '22'

      - name: Count eval cases
        id: count
        run: |
          N=$(ls evals/golden-trajectories/*.yaml 2>/dev/null | wc -l)
          echo "count=$N" >> $GITHUB_OUTPUT
          echo "Found $N golden trajectories"

      # v3.13 SSOT gate（SOTA team 审计 R1）：COUNTS.md 自称唯一计数源但一直无 enforcer
      # → 计数漂移（hooks 写 9 实 10）。check-counts.sh 比对 COUNTS vs 文件系统，不符 exit 1。
      # 同时硬查 5 个安全红线 guard 存在（防 cto-init 安装链断裂复发）。
      - name: Check counts SSOT (R1)
        run: |
          chmod +x scripts/check-counts.sh
          bash scripts/check-counts.sh

      # v3.12 核心 gate（飞轮第 7-8 轮 team 修「铁律 #12 eval 空壳」）：
      # 旧 CI 只 count yaml + assert 字段存在 = §32.5 反模式 #6 eval-gaming。
      # 现在真跑每个 verification_command（hook 红线 / guard 行为），FAIL → exit 1 阻 merge。
      # AlphaEvolve evaluator-grounded：eval 必须真执行才是 fitness 函数。
      # v4.0e: guard engine 单测（node:test）纳入 gate — 引擎红线逻辑回归防护。
      - name: Run guard engine unit tests
        run: |
          if [ -f .claude/hooks/engine/guard.test.mjs ]; then
            node --test .claude/hooks/engine/guard.test.mjs
          else
            echo "no engine unit suite — skipped"
          fi

      - name: Run executable evals (real execution)
        run: |
          chmod +x scripts/run-evals.sh
          bash scripts/run-evals.sh

      - name: Validate yaml structure
        run: |
          python3 - <<'PY'

codex
The patch activates configuration that cannot invoke its hooks portably, fails the repository's count gate, and contains reproducible guard bypasses. It also violates the project's mandatory eval requirement for agent configuration changes.

Full review comments:

- [P1] Match the tool names Codex actually emits — C:\projects\ai-playbook\.codex\hooks.json:5-5
  In a Codex session these Claude-only matchers (`Edit`, `Write`, `MultiEdit`, and later `Bash`) never match Codex file or shell tools, so none of the forbidden-path, branch, test-lock, destructive-command, or eval guards run. Map the actual Codex tool names/events before relying on these hooks for the protections required by [AGENTS.md:34-35](AGENTS.md#L34-L35).

- [P1] Resolve hook scripts relative to the checkout — C:\projects\ai-playbook\.codex\hooks.json:9-9
  For every clone not located at `C:\projects\ai-playbook`, each hook tries to execute a nonexistent script in the author's checkout, disabling all enforcement. Build commands from the repository root or a portable relative path instead of embedding machine-specific configuration, as required by [AGENTS.md:31](AGENTS.md#L31).

- [P1] Keep the shared skill count consistent with its SSOT — C:\projects\ai-playbook\.agents\skills\constitution-loader\SKILL.md:1-1
  Adding these ten `.agents/skills` directories raises the actual count from 6 to 16 without updating `docs/ai-cto/COUNTS.md`; `bash scripts/check-counts.sh` consequently exits 1 with `skills (.agents): 实际 16 ≠ ... 6`, and the eval workflow runs that hard gate whenever `.agents/skills/**` changes. Update the SSOT or avoid adding duplicate skills.

- [P1] Limit the echo carve-out to the whole command — C:\projects\ai-playbook\.codex\hooks\engine\guards.mjs:515-516
  When a multiline shell input contains any line beginning with `echo` or `printf` and no listed operator, this condition exits before scanning later lines; for example, `echo ok\nrm -rf /` is allowed with exit 0. Require the entire input to be a single output-only command rather than using a multiline match.

- [P1] Validate the destination of MCP move operations — C:\projects\ai-playbook\.codex\hooks\engine\guards.mjs:568-569
  For `mcp__filesystem__move_file`, this branch validates only `ctx.filePath` and ignores the already-parsed `ctx.mcpDest`. Moving a safe source to `docs/ai-cto/CONSTITUTION.md`, a forbidden path, or a locked test path therefore exits 0 and bypasses the protections required by [AGENTS.md:34-35](AGENTS.md#L34-L35); run the same checks against both source and destination.

- [P1] Add golden trajectories for the new agent configuration — C:\projects\ai-playbook\.codex\agents\eval-runner.toml:1-2
  This commit adds multiple agent and skill configurations but no case under `evals/golden-trajectories/` exercises their Codex discovery, paths, or behavior; the new Node suite only tests copied guard functions. That violates the explicit no-eval-no-main gate in [AGENTS.md:33](AGENTS.md#L33), so add behavior-level trajectories before merging.

- [P2] Match test directories at the repository root — C:\projects\ai-playbook\.codex\hooks\engine\guards.mjs:395-395
  The directory alternatives require a preceding slash, so files such as `tests/case.yaml` and `test/case.yaml` produce no Test-Lock reminder unless their filename independently matches a suffix pattern. Use an `(^|/)tests?/` boundary so the root-level test directories covered by [AGENTS.md:35](AGENTS.md#L35) are included.

- [P2] Route Anthropic models to the correct executor — C:\projects\ai-playbook\AGENTS.md:41-43
  The table assigns Opus, Sonnet, and Haiku to Codex even though they are Anthropic models, so agents following this root instruction receive an impossible routing plan. Restore Claude Code as the executor for these rows to remain consistent with the Claude-native platform contract in [docs/ai-cto/CONSTITUTION.md:17](docs/ai-cto/CONSTITUTION.md#L17).

- [P2] Point instructions at the committed Codex layout — C:\projects\ai-playbook\AGENTS.md:78-81
  These instructions describe `.Codex/settings.json`, commands, rules, and skills that are not present in the committed tree, while the new files are under lowercase `.codex`; on case-sensitive systems even the directory name differs. Agents following this inventory will skip the actual configuration and report nonexistent components, so document the real `.codex/config.toml`, hooks, and agent paths.
